In this work, we observe that online balancing can instead be largely overlapped with computation before target routing (e.g., attention), if routing distributions can be predicted accurately in advance. Therefore, we propose FreeBalance, a lossless online load-balancing framework that overlaps expert migration with preceding computation stages via residual workload prediction. FreeBalance leverages cross-layer similarities in hidden representations within the residual network to build a lightweight workload predictor. This enables proactive expert migration planning before routing decisions are available, creating substantial overlap between weight transfer and computation-heavy pre-routing stages. Furthermore, a cost model constrains the number of swaps to fully hide the synchronization overhead within the available window. Experiments across models and datasets show that FreeBalance reduces the max-to-mean rank load ratio by 32.8% and end-to-end prefill latency by 13.1%. Specifically, our method hides balancing overhead of an average of 5.1 experts per layer, which would otherwise account for about 8.5% of the critical-path latency.

</details>


### [98] [APTER: Adaptive Post-Training with Expert-Grounded Rubrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14212)
*Xukai Wang,Liangqi Li,Zhiyue Xu,Jingang Zhou,Xiaoyu Shi,Jiansheng Cai,Bo Zhang,Zhe Li,Xu-Yao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出APTER框架，将专家构建的结构化标准体系整合到细粒度的评估、优化与诊断中，用于专业复杂推理任务的后训练，在数学推理和医学问答上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型进入专业领域时需满足领域约束、包含关键证据并提供完整推理，但现有后训练方法依赖整体偏好或结果级验证，而基于评分规则的方法通常为每个查询独立生成规则，缺乏结构化的专家知识约束，导致关键要求遗漏和跨样本不一致，难以诊断和修复持续的能力缺陷。

Method: APTER包含两个核心阶段：(1) 专家接地评分规则构建——从领域专家建立的标准框架出发，每个标准代表稳定的专业能力，对每个查询选择相关标准并实例化为查询级评分规则，将可复用的专家标准转化为无需参考答案的查询级监督；(2) 自适应后训练——将评分规则判定同时作为优化信号和标准级诊断信号，按标准ID聚合低分判定以揭示持续缺陷，并在强化学习过程中触发针对性的监督微调更新。

Result: 在数学推理和医学问答两个领域及三代模型上均取得一致提升，数学和医学平均分相对对应基座模型最高分别提升15.86和8.04分。

Conclusion: 将专家结构化领域知识融入基于评分规则的后训练，能够有效提升专业领域复杂推理能力，并通过标准级诊断实现针对性的能力缺陷修复。

Abstract: As large language models enter professional domains, they must satisfy domain constraints, include critical evidence, and provide complete reasoning rather than merely produce fluent responses. Existing post-training methods often rely on holistic preferences or outcome-level verification, while recent rubric-based methods usually generate rubrics independently for each query. In specialized domains, such unconstrained rubrics may omit critical requirements and vary across samples, hindering the diagnosis and targeted repair of persistent capability deficiencies. We propose APTER (Adaptive Post-Training with Expert-Grounded Rubrics), a framework that integrates structured domain knowledge into fine-grained evaluation, optimization, and diagnosis for specialized complex reasoning. First, expert-grounded rubric construction starts from an expert criteria framework built by domain experts, where each criterion represents a stable professional capability. For each query, APTER selects relevant criteria and instantiates them into query-level rubrics linked to their source criteria, turning reusable expert criteria into executable query-level supervision without reference answers. Second, adaptive post-training uses rubric verdicts as both optimization and criterion-level diagnostic signals. Aggregating low-scoring verdicts by criterion ID reveals persistent deficiencies and triggers targeted supervised fine-tuning updates during reinforcement learning. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and medical question answering show consistent gains across both domains. Across three model generations, APTER improves the mathematics and medical averages over the corresponding base models by up to 15.86 and 8.04 points, respectively. Code and rubric datasets are available at https://github.com/AntDT-APTER/APTER.

</details>


### [99] [A Generalized Parallelogram Rule for Proportional Analogies on Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14220)
*Pierre-Alexandre Murena,Marcelo Hartmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了黎曼流形上的比例类比关系，将欧几里得空间中用于算术类比的平行四边形规则扩展到非欧几里得空间，并在球面、形状空间和概率分布流形等流形上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比例类比在符号域和向量空间中已有较多研究，但在非欧几里得空间中的使用受到限制。为了填补这一空白，作者希望在黎曼流形上建立比例类比关系。

Method: 将欧几里得空间中用于算术类比的平行四边形规则扩展到黎曼流形上，从而在黎曼域中引入比例类比关系。

Result: 在球面、形状空间以及概率分布流形等多种流形上展示了所引入类比关系的有效性。

Conclusion: 成功在黎曼几何框架下定义了比例类比，拓宽了类比推理在非欧几里得空间中的应用范围。

Abstract: Analogies are quaternary relations of the form "a is to b as c is to d", usually denoted a : b :: c : d. This notion is formalized in particular with the notion of proportional analogy, which imposes some constraints on the valid analogies. Whereas proportional analogies have been studied mostly in symbolic domains and in vector spaces, their use is limited in non-Euclidean spaces. In this paper, we introduce a proportional analogy relation in Riemannian domains, extending the parallelogram rule used for arithmetic analogies in Euclidean spaces. We illustrate the introduced analogy on various manifolds, such as the sphere, shape spaces and manifolds of probability distributions.

</details>


### [100] [Attributing Preprocessing Invariance in Spectral Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14227)
*Dongjun Wei,Hongyi Wu,Yinuo Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文质疑了谱域基础模型"预处理不变性"作为学习证据的解读。以拉曼基础模型为例，指出其归一化层本身就能消除某些预处理差异（频谱的正倍数加常数），该不变性不需要任何可学习参数。在六个拉曼数据集上，模型并未显著优于其自身的归一化层；训练虽能提升编码器，但对变换的不变性仅在该变换到达编码器时才被学习。跨五种模态的多个系统中，多数归一化已能消除此类变换，却被误报为"学习到的不变性"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上用"一个预处理下训练、另一个预处理下测试"的准确率保持来衡量谱域基础模型的预处理不变性，但作者认为这种度量可能被模型自身的归一化层混淆——归一化若已将不同预处理谱映射到相同向量，则不变性并非来自学习。需要重新审视这一衡量标准。

Method: 以拉曼基础模型为案例，分析其归一化层（基于每个频谱自身统计量的归一化）何时会将两个不同预处理的谱映射到同一向量——即一个谱是另一个谱的正倍数加常数。在六个拉曼数据集上对比模型与归一化单独的性能；通过受控实验测试编码器是否真正学到忽略某种变换；用数值测试判定给定归一化消除哪些变换；并在五种模态的已发布系统中复现该对比。

Result: 模型在六个拉曼数据集上未可测量地优于其自身归一化；归一化单独就能改进原始谱。训练确实提升编码器（优于随机初始化），但受控实验表明编码器仅在变换到达它时才学会忽略该变换。跨模态审查发现多数系统的归一化已能消除"正倍数加常数"类变换，且多系统将其误报为学习到的不变性；在两个系统的复现中也未观察到学习增益。

Conclusion: 谱域基础模型中报告的大部分预处理不变性可能源自归一化层而非学习参数。评估此类模型时，应以归一化本身为基线对比，否则会高估模型的学习能力。

Abstract: Preprocessing invariance is an appealing goal for spectral foundation models: a frozen model should remain useful when laboratories preprocess spectra differently. It is usually measured by training a classifier under one preprocessing pipeline and testing it under another, with preserved accuracy read as evidence of learning. We revisit that reading, using a Raman foundation model as a case study. Such models normalize their inputs before any learned parameter is applied. If that normalization maps two differently preprocessed spectra to the same vector, the encoder receives identical inputs, so the invariance cannot be attributed to learning. For a normalization that uses each spectrum's own statistics, this happens exactly when one spectrum is a positive multiple of the other plus a constant. Several standard preprocessing operations take that form. The encoder should therefore be measured against the normalization alone, which has no learned parameters. On six Raman evaluation datasets, the model does not measurably outperform its own normalization. It improves on raw spectra, but so does the normalization alone. Training does improve the encoder over random initialization, and a controlled experiment shows that it learns to ignore a transformation only when that transformation reaches it. A numerical test settles which transformations a given normalization removes. Across released systems in five modalities, most normalizations already remove transformations of that form, and several of those systems claim that invariance as learned. Replicating the comparison on two of them shows no gain either.

</details>


### [101] [Polaris : Multi Agentic System for Conversational Enterprise Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14246)
*Varuni H K,Soham Sarkar,Jay Kumar,Goutham Krishnan,Tanvi Johari,Avinash Bharadwaj,Santosh Hegde*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Polaris，一个监督式多智能体框架，用于会话式企业级分析。其核心是动态任务协调（DTC）机制，将智能体-任务分配建模为自适应二分图匹配，结合推理优先的ReAct风格智能体，将自然语言查询转化为连贯的分析工作流，实现数据检索、可视化和"为什么"层面的解释性推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代企业面临"数据丰富但洞察贫乏"的困境：查询、解释和沟通企业级规模信息的复杂性严重制约了组织从数据中快速获取洞察的能力。现有的自然语言查询和可视化方案缺乏对复杂分析流程的协调与推理能力，无法提供端到端的可信业务智能。

Method: Polaris包含三个核心组件：1) 监督者（supervisor）协调机制；2) 动态任务协调（DTC）——一种基于决策理论的编排层，将智能体-任务分配形式化为自适应二分图匹配，支持实时协调、故障恢复和全局优化；3) 推理优先（reason-first）的ReAct风格智能体，分别负责查询、可视化和推理，将自然语言查询转化为连贯的分析工作流。

Result: 在结构化企业数据集上的评估表明，Polaris实现了高语义保真度和答案相关性，验证了多智能体编排在规模化交付可信、端到端业务智能方面的潜力。

Conclusion: 多智能体编排是解决企业级会话分析的关键路径，监督式协调与决策理论驱动的任务分配能够显著提升自然语言到分析工作流转换的质量，为规模化业务智能提供可信的端到端解决方案。

Abstract: In today's fast-paced environment, the ability to swiftly access, understand, and act on data is no longer optional; it is essential. Yet most organizations remain data-rich but insight-poor, constrained by the complexity of querying, interpreting, and explaining enterprise-scale information. We present Polaris, a supervisor-led multi-agent framework for conversational enterprise analytics that bridges this gap. Polaris introduces Dynamic Task Coordination (DTC), a decision-theoretic orchestration layer that models agent-task assignment as adaptive bipartite matching, enabling real-time coordination, recovery, and optimization across specialized agents for querying, visualization, and reasoning. By coupling DTC with reason-first, ReAct-style agents, Polaris transforms natural-language queries into coherent analytical workflows that not only retrieve and visualize data but also explain the underlying "why." Evaluation on structured enterprise datasets demonstrates high semantic fidelity and answer relevancy, underscoring the potential of multi-agent orchestration to deliver trustworthy, end-to-end business intelligence at scale.

</details>


### [102] [TimeSage-EV: A Live Benchmark for Agentic Time Series Analysis in Evolving Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14270)
*Qingren Yao,Yaxuan Kong,Yuqi Nie,Yichen Li,Stefan Zohren,Anna Vettoruzzo,Qingsong Wen,Ming Jin,Joaquin Vanschoren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TimeSage-EV是一个面向演化环境中智能体时间序列分析的实时基准测试，包含60个真实机构场景、1,485个情景-周期问答对，跨6个领域和不同的数据发布节奏。实验表明前沿LLM智能体在时间有效性、外生背景利用和适应性方面存在显著性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列QA基准大多依赖固定快照数据，未能评估时间有效性（temporal validity）和截止日期感知（cutoff-aware）的证据使用。然而高风险领域的时间序列分析依赖于周期性数据发布，新观测值可能改变证据基础并影响结论的有效性，因此需要一个能评估智能体在动态演化环境中分析能力的基准。

Method: 构建TimeSage-EV实时基准，追踪60个真实机构场景、6个领域，包含2023年2月至2026年5月的1,485个情景-周期QA对，覆盖月度、周度、日度和不规则发布节奏。在每个周期，LLM智能体接收时间序列数据和来源报告，同时扣留目标发布作为ground truth。基准评估三项能力：状态识别（state identification）、数据摘要（data summarization）和展望推理（outlook reasoning）。此外提出TimeSage-1.0，一种具有可复用分析技能库的自演化智能体。

Result: 对前沿LLM智能体和TimeSage-1.0的实验揭示了不同模型层级间的显著性能差距，并发现反复出现的失败模式：时间有效性判断错误、外生背景信息利用不足以及适应性不足。TimeSage-1.0作为自演化智能体在该基准上具有一定优势。

Conclusion: TimeSage-EV揭示了现有LLM智能体在演化数据环境下的时间序列分析能力不足，并作为一个持续更新的研究资源（含月度更新、代码、排行榜和失败模式分析）开放，推动时间序列分析智能体的时序有效性和动态适应能力研究。

Abstract: Time series analysis in high-stakes domains relies on recurring data releases, where new observations can alter the evidence base and the validity of later conclusions. Existing time series QA benchmarks mostly rely on fixed snapshots, leaving temporal validity and cutoff-aware evidence use unevaluated. We introduce TimeSage-EV, a live benchmark for agentic time series analysis in evolving environments. It tracks 60 real institutional scenarios across 6 domains, comprising 1,485 scenario-period QA pairs from Feb 2023 to May 2026 and spanning monthly, weekly, daily, and irregular release cadences. At each period, large language model (LLM) agents receive time series data and source reports, while the withheld target release provides ground truth. TimeSage-EV evaluates state identification, data summarization, and outlook reasoning. Experiments with frontier LLM agents and TimeSage-1.0, a novel self-evolving agent with a reusable analytical skill library, reveal significant performance gaps across model tiers and recurring failures in temporal validity, exogenous context use, and adaptation. We release TimeSage-EV as a research resource with monthly updates, code, a leaderboard, and failure-mode analyses.

</details>


### [103] [Intern-S2-Mobius: Foundation Model with Decoupled Knowledge and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14290)
*Kai Chen,Jifeng Ding,Ning Ding,Jiaye Ge,Lixin Gu,Yicheng Gu,Qipeng Guo,Ermo Hua,Haian Huang,Haozheng Hou,Jie Hou,Xiangyu Hong,Che Jiang,Minxi Jin,Cheng Liang,Dahua Lin,Dawei Liu,Kuikun Liu,Chengqi Lv,Haijun Lv,Han Lv,Ningsheng Ma,Biqing Qi,Jianmin Qian,Shiya Su,Youbang Sun,Huanze Tang,Zhongbo Tian,Hanjing Wang,Rui Wang,Ting Wang,Yi Wang,Baiting Wu,Jun Xu,Bowen Yang,Hui Wang,Weida Wang,Haochen Ye,Jiashuo Yu,Shan Yu,Xiaoyi Yu,Qirui Zeng,Qi Zhang,Ming Zhang,Wenwei Zhang,Bowen Zhou,Xinyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Mobius-v0是一种将知识存储（共享FFN内存）与组合推理（多个Self-Attn推理器）分离的架构，通过隐藏状态作为缓存载体实现知识查询与推理的迭代交互。7B模型从零训练仅需62.6%的数据即可达到Transformer基线水平，35B持续预训练模型在保持下游性能的同时实现近4倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer架构将知识存储与推理计算耦合在同一层中，导致知识压缩效率低且推理计算开销大。作者旨在通过架构层面的知识-推理分离，实现更高效的知识压缩和推理速度提升。

Method: 提出Mobius-v0架构：包含一个全局共享的Memory（即FFN模块）用于存储知识向量，以及多个Reasoners（即Self-Attention模块）用于迭代执行组合推理。推理器以隐藏状态作为缓存和载体，反复向内存查询所需的知识向量，知识再传回推理算子，形成知识查询-推理的迭代循环。

Result: 1）从零训练的7B模型在相同下游分数下仅需Transformer基线62.6%的训练数据；2）基于Qwen3.5-35B持续预训练的Intern-S2-Mobius，在下游分数相当的情况下实现了近4倍的端到端推理加速。

Conclusion: 知识-推理分离架构Mobius-v0在训练数据效率和推理效率上均显著优于传统Transformer基线，验证了将知识记忆与组合推理解耦是一种有效的架构设计方向。

Abstract: We introduce Mobius-v0, an architecture that comprises a globally shared Memory (FFN) that stores knowledge vectors and multiple Reasoners (Self-Attn) that iteratively achieve compositional reasoning. Using hidden states as cache and carrier, reasoners repeatedly query memory for required knowledge-vectors, while the knowledge is transmitted back to reasoning operators. Through this knowledge-reasoning-separation architecture, Mobius achieves better knowledge compression and reasoning efficiency. Built upon Mobius-v0 architecture: 1) Our 7B model trained-from-scratch achieves similar downstream score as a 7B Transformer baseline with 62.6% of baseline's training data. 2) Our Intern-S2-Mobius, continually-pretrained from Qwen3.5-35B, achieves similar downstream score while delivering nearly 4x end-to-end inference speedup.

</details>


### [104] [Sensor-Driven Mission Synthesis for UAV/UGV Swarms: A TB-CSPN Coordination Architecture with Hardware-Enforced Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14306)
*Uwe M. Borghoff,Paolo Bottoni,Remo Pareschi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种异构无人机/无人地面车辆集群协调架构，结合TB-CSPN编排与独立模拟安全包络，在保持硬件级执行安全的前提下，从不确定的多模态传感器证据中合成任务动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构UAV/UGV集群在对抗性、不确定环境中运行时面临多模态传感器证据不确定、通信受限/被干扰/被欺骗等多重挑战，现有方法难以同时保证任务形成的灵活性、协调层的可审计性以及执行层的物理安全性。

Method: 采用Topic-Based Communication Space Petri Net (TB-CSPN)进行协调编排；咨询智能体将雷达、射频、声学和视觉等传感器输出转化为带时间界限的语义令牌；监督智能体负责授权和策略管控的任务转换发布；协调层通过守卫和同步机制约束非确定性；在数字协调层之外引入独立的模拟安全包络，对发送给单个车辆的作动器指令进行钳位或否决。

Result: 通过沿海监视案例研究展示了该架构能够在运行不确定性下实现可靠、受治理且物理安全的集群协调；分离解释、协调与执行三个层次产生可审计的决策路径，并支持异构证据的有界时间集成。

Conclusion: 该架构通过解释-协调-执行三层分离与模拟安全包络的双层保障机制，实现了对抗环境下集群任务的增量形成与物理安全之间的平衡，为异构无人系统集群的安全自主协调提供了可行的架构方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a coordination architecture for heterogeneous UAV/UGV swarms that synthesises mission actions from uncertain, multi-modal sensor evidence while preserving hardware-enforced safety at the actuation boundary. The approach combines radar, RF, acoustic, and visual observations with Topic-Based Communication Space Petri Net (TB-CSPN) orchestration to support incremental mission formation under partial and evolving information. Consultant agents transform sensor outputs into temporally bounded semantic tokens, while supervisor agents provide authorisation and policy-governed release of mission transitions. This separation between interpretation, coordination, and execution yields auditable decision paths, constrains non-determinism within the coordination layer through guards and synchronisation, and enables bounded-time integration of heterogeneous evidence. To improve resilience in contested environments, including cyber compromise, spoofing, jamming, and communication loss, the digital coordination layer is complemented by independent analogue safety envelopes that clamp or veto unsafe actuator commands issued to individual vehicles. A coastal-surveillance case study illustrates how the proposed architecture enables dependable, governed, and physically safe swarm coordination under operational uncertainty.

</details>


### [105] [Program-space Diffusion for Morphology-to-Transcriptomics Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14330)
*Ruyter Swann,Dorent Reuben,Racoceanu Daniel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将形态学到转录组的预测重新定义为转录程序空间中的条件生成任务，利用共识非负矩阵分解（cNMF）提取低维转录程序，并训练条件扩散模型从组织学图像生成程序激活值，从而利用协调的转录变异并大幅降低条件生成任务的维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学成本高、可扩展性有限，现有从组织学预测空间表达的模型多在基因水平操作，未利用成熟的转录组建模实践，且依赖异质的基因选择策略导致方法间公平比较困难。

Method: 使用共识非负矩阵分解（cNMF）从训练数据中提取一组捕获协调表达变异的低维转录程序，然后训练条件扩散模型从组织学图像生成程序激活值，将形态学到转录组的预测重构为转录程序空间中的条件生成任务。

Result: 该公式化方法利用了协调的转录变异，并显著降低了条件生成任务的维度（结果部分未在摘要中详细展开，但核心优势在于低维转录程序空间中的条件生成）。

Conclusion: 通过将预测从基因水平提升到转录程序水平，该方法在保持生成质量的同时降低了任务维度，为形态学到转录组的预测提供了一种更统一、可比较的框架。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) enables genome-wide gene expression profiling while preserving tissue architecture, but its cost and limited scalability remain major bottlenecks. This has motivated models that predict spatial expression directly from routine histology. Despite promising results, most existing approaches operate at the gene level without leveraging established transcriptomic modeling practices and rely on heterogeneous gene selection strategies, which complicates fair comparison across methods.
  We propose to reformulate morphology-to-transcriptomics prediction as conditional generation in transcriptional program space, thereby exploiting coordinated transcriptional variation instead of predicting genes independently. Using consensus non-negative matrix factorization (cNMF), we extract a low-dimensional set of transcriptional programs capturing coordinated expression variation in the training data, and train a conditional diffusion model to generate program activations from histology. This formulation exploits coordinated transcriptional variation and substantially lowers the dimensionality of the conditional generative task.

</details>


### [106] [Clearing the Fog: Towards Installing and Refining Proactive Exploration Capabilities in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14339)
*Zhizhao Guan,Chen Huang,Ziming Liu,Hongru Liang,Wenqiang Lei,See-Kiong Ng,Tat-Seng Chua,Anthony G Cohn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体的主动探索能力，提出SAFARI方法，包含探索性数据构建（缓解标准演示的后见之明偏差）和带对比信号引导的RL优化（区分有效探索与冗余游荡）两个组件，实验证明了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体需要具备主动探索环境以获取信息来改进未来决策的能力，但目前这一能力存在两个瓶颈：标准演示中存在后见之明（hindsight bias），以及缺乏有效区分有益探索和无效游荡的优化信号。

Method: 提出SAFARI方法，包含两个核心组件：(1) 探索性数据构建——合成包含丰富探索行为的轨迹，以缓解标准演示中的后见之明偏差；(2) 对比信号引导的RL优化——利用对比轨迹对来区分生产性探索与冗余游荡。

Result: 大量实验验证了SAFARI在增强LLM智能体主动探索能力方面的有效性，并为主动探索的特征提供了深入见解。

Conclusion: SAFARI通过合成探索性数据与对比信号引导的强化学习相结合，有效提升了LLM智能体的主动探索能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: We study proactive exploration in LLM agents, i.e., the ability to explore an environment to acquire information that improves future decision-making. In this regard, we first identify two fundamental bottlenecks that hinder this capability and then propose \ours, a novel method designed to instill and refine proactive exploration. Specifically, \ours\ consists of two components: (1) Exploratory Data Construction, which synthesizes exploration-rich trajectories to mitigate the hindsight bias of standard demonstrations; and (2) RL Optimization with Contrastive Signal Guidance, which leverages contrastive trajectory pairs to distinguish productive exploration from redundant wandering. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of \ours\ and provide insights into the characteristics of proactive exploration. Our code is available at: https://github.com/GuanZhizhao/SAFARI.

</details>


### [107] [ScienceFlow: A long-horizon agent for ML research, scientific discovery and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14354)
*Mingming Zhao,Jiqian Dong,Kangping Xu,Zadid Hasan,Chengrui Fan,Shan Jiang,Shuai Mao,Ting Lingya,Linyi Zou,Tailin Zhou,Yun Hin Chan,Wenkai Zhang,Zhanhong Zhou,Guowei Huang,Hongliang Li,Wenjing Cun,Zhitang Chen,Mingxuan Yuan,Yanhui Geng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ScienceFlow是一个端到端自动研究智能体框架，通过将长时程研究组织为可执行工作区中的研究片段、采用可恢复的执行状态表示研究进度，并引入ESTRA（可执行状态转移与重新锚定）机制和证据感知的执行控制器，实现高效的探索、修订和执行。在MLE-bench上取得70.22%的Any-Medal分数（SOTA），超越此前最优结果4.92个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动研究智能体虽然取得了成功，但缺乏连续性机制、无法从死胡同中恢复，也缺少基于价值的计算资源分配，这从根本上损害了搜索效率、浪费计算资源并降低了最终成功概率。该工作旨在弥合这一差距，实现长时程、稳定且目标对齐的自主研究。

Method: ScienceFlow框架包含三个核心设计：（1）将长时程研究组织为基于可执行工作区的研究片段，并将研究进度表示为可恢复的执行状态，支持高效探索、修订和执行；（2）提出可执行状态转移通过重新锚定（ESTRA）机制，在每个研究片段间的转移决策中，选择活跃状态或存档状态作为下一个锚点，并决定研究轨迹是继续还是重定向；（3）引入证据感知的执行控制器，根据资源可用性、剩余预算和已验证的进度为物理任务分配计算资源。

Result: 在机器学习、科学建模和数学优化等多样化的长时程基准上评估ScienceFlow。在24小时预算下于完整MLE-bench上取得了70.22%的Any-Medal分数（SOTA），超越了此前报告的最好结果4.92个百分点，展示了其维持有效研究过程的能力。

Conclusion: ScienceFlow的有效性表明，高效的状态管理、自适应探索和目标对齐的执行对于将自主研究扩展到短时程交互之外至关重要。高效的状态管理、自适应探索与目标对齐的执行是推动自主研究规模化发展的关键因素。

Abstract: Enabling LLM agents to sustain productive, stable, and goal-aligned research over extended horizons is a central challenge for autonomous machine learning and scientific discovery, as progress hinges on continuously managing evolving state, exploration decisions, and computational resources. Pioneering autoresearch agents, despite great success, still lack mechanisms for continuity, recovery from dead ends, and value-driven compute allocation, which inherently undermines overall search efficiency, wastes computational resources, and lowers the chance of ultimate success. To bridge this gap, we introduce ScienceFlow, an end-to-end autoresearch agent framework that organizes long-horizon research work into research segments grounded in executable workspaces. It represents research progress as recoverable executable states, enabling efficient exploration, revision, and execution. Transitions between research segments are governed by Executable-State Transition through Re-Anchoring (ESTRA), which selects either the live state or an archived state as the next anchor and determines whether to continue or redirect the research trajectory. An evidence-aware execution controller allocates resources to physical jobs based on resource availability, remaining budget, and validated progress. We evaluate ScienceFlow on tasks spanning machine learning, scientific modeling, and mathematical optimization. Results on diverse long-horizon benchmarks demonstrate its ability to sustain effective research processes, highlighted by a SOTA 70.22 percent Any-Medal score on the full MLE-bench within a 24-hour budget, outperforming prior reported results by 4.92 percentage points. The efficacy of ScienceFlow further demonstrates that efficient state management, adaptive exploration, and objective-aligned execution are critical for scaling autonomous research beyond short-horizon interactions.

</details>


### [108] [Disentangled Shared Representations Improve Morpho-Transcriptomic Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14355)
*Julian Ostermaier,Swann Ruyter,Reuben Dorent,Daniel Racoceanu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究系统比较了标准与解耦变体的VAE和对比学习方法在多模态空间转录组学表示学习中的表现，发现对比目标在下游探针性能上更优，而显式解耦共享与私有成分能改善重建和探针指标，但收益取决于模型家族、任务方向和解耦强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学可同时获取基因表达和组织形态信息，但标准多模态模型往往将所有模态压缩到同一潜在空间，未显式区分共享与模态特有的变异来源，可能限制下游任务的实用性。

Method: 在匹配实验条件下，对两个癌症队列数据，比较VAE类与对比学习类方法的标准变体和解耦变体，分别从跨模态重建、下游探针和跨模态探针迁移三个维度评估表示质量。

Result: 两个主要发现：其一，对比学习目标的下游探针性能优于VAE类模型；其二，解耦变体在重建和探针指标上有所提升，但提升幅度依赖模型家族、任务、方向和特定解耦强度的配置。

Conclusion: 显式分解共享与模态特有信息可以改善空间转录组学的多模态表示学习，同时该评估框架可为未来基础模型提供有价值的基准参考。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) enables the simultaneous profiling of gene expression and tissue morphology, creating an opportunity to learn multimodal representations capturing shared morpho-transcriptomic structure. However, standard multimodal models often compress modalities into a common latent space without explicitly separating shared and modality-specific sources of variation, which may limit downstream utility. We investigate whether explicit disentanglement of shared and private latent components improves multimodal representation learning for paired Hematoxylin \& Eosin (H\&E) and ST data. We compare VAE-based and contrastive approaches, each in standard and disentangled variants, across two cancer cohorts under matched experimental conditions. Representations are evaluated using cross-modal reconstruction, downstream probing and cross-modal probe transfer. The experiments suggest two main trends. First, contrastive objectives yield higher downstream probing performance than VAE-based models. Second, disentangled variants improve the selected reconstruction and probing metrics, although the gains depend on the model family, task, direction, and disentanglement strength. Overall, our results suggest that explicitly factorizing shared and modality-specific information can improve multimodal representation learning for spatial transcriptomics and provides a useful evaluation framework for future foundation models.

</details>


### [109] [Designing Sustainable Federated Learning as a Service using Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14359)
*Keya Patel,Sajib Mistry,Sheik Fattah,Deepak Kanneganti,Aneesh Krishna,Mufti Mahmud,Monowar Bhuyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SFLaaS，一个面向碳约束联邦学习服务的神经架构搜索框架，通过需求驱动的搜索空间、消费者级碳可行性评估、可持续消费者调度策略和进化搜索策略，在硬碳约束下实现预测性能、消费者可行性和参与覆盖率的联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FLaaS环境中的可持续性约束给维护碳可行的联邦训练带来重大挑战，这些约束常导致消费者参与不可行和硬碳约束下的联邦训练不稳定，亟需一个能在碳约束下保证训练稳定的框架。

Method: 提出SFLaaS框架，包含四个核心组件：(1)需求驱动的搜索空间，将消费者可持续性画像转化为可行的架构区域；(2)消费者级碳可行性评估机制，在动态碳条件下评估候选架构；(3)可持续消费者调度策略，自适应选择可行消费者并分配本地工作负载；(4)进化搜索策略，在硬碳约束下联合优化预测性能、消费者可行性和参与覆盖率。

Result: 在真实数据集和模拟环境上的实验验证了所提出方法的有效性。

Conclusion: SFLaaS通过将消费者可持续性约束纳入联邦学习架构搜索的各个环节，能够在碳约束下维持消费者参与和统计数据覆盖率，实现碳可行的联邦训练。

Abstract: The sustainability constraints of FLaaS consumers pose significant challenges to maintaining carbon-feasible federated training in FLaaS environments. These constraints often lead to infeasible consumer participation and unstable federated training under hard carbon constraints. We propose a Sustainable Federated Learning as a Service (SFLaaS), a carbon- constrained Neural Architecture Search (NAS) framework for heteroge- neous sustainable constraints. We introduce a requirement-driven search space that transforms consumer sustainability profiles into a feasible architecture region before federated execution. We develop a consumer-level carbon feasibility estimation mechanism to evaluate candidate architectures under dynamic carbon conditions. We propose a sustainable con- sumer scheduling strategy that adaptively selects feasible consumers and allocates local workloads to preserve consumer participation and statistical data coverage. An evolutionary search strategy jointly optimised for predictive performance, consumer feasibility, and participation coverage under hard carbon constraints. Experiments on real-world datasets and a simulated environment demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [110] [AgentRewind: Recoverable Execution for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14380)
*Yu Zhuang,Kefei Chen,Yitong Duan,Shuxin Zheng,Jian Li,Xu-Yao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentRewind是一个运行时恢复框架，通过记录智能体上下文和受控环境的对齐检查点，允许长时间执行中的LLM智能体回退到早期状态并从之前尝试中恢复执行。作者还构建了MettleBench基准，用于评估长时间工程任务中的完成率和部分进展。实验表明AgentRewind在任务成功率和平均清单进度上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长时间执行环境中，早期错误会通过智能体上下文和环境状态传播，且难以通过后续操作逆转。现有方法主要在错误发生前通过计划细化和安全检查来减少错误，但缺少错误发生后的恢复支持。

Method: AgentRewind框架记录智能体上下文与受控环境的对齐检查点，使智能体能够回退到早期状态并利用之前尝试的信息恢复执行。同时构建MettleBench基准，包含一系列相关需求的长时间工程任务，用于评估任务完成率和部分进展。

Result: 在多种任务、多个模型、不同执行策略和智能体框架上的实验表明，AgentRewind相比基线方法显著提升了任务成功率和平均清单进度。

Conclusion: AgentRewind通过运行时状态回退机制有效支持LLM智能体在长时间执行中的错误恢复，为长时程智能体执行提供了实用的恢复能力，弥补了现有方法在错误发生后缺乏支持的不足。

Abstract: Many real-world tasks require LLM agents to interact with their environments over long execution horizons. Errors that occur early in execution may propagate through both the agent context and environment state, and their effects may be difficult to reverse through subsequent actions. Existing methods mainly seek to reduce such errors through plan refinement and safety checks but provide little support after errors occur. To enable recovery during long-horizon execution, we present AgentRewind, a runtime recovery framework that records aligned checkpoints of the agent context and controlled environment, allowing agents to return to an earlier state and resume execution with information from previous attempts. We also construct MettleBench, a benchmark for evaluating task completion and partial progress on long-horizon engineering assignments containing a series of related requirements. Experiments across tasks, multiple models, execution strategies, and agent harnesses show that AgentRewind improves task success rate and average checklist progress over the compared baselines.

</details>


### [111] [Tripwire: Triggering Aligned Refusal via Statistically Certified Safety Neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14392)
*Wei Zhao,Zhe Li,Peixin Zhang,Jun Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TriPwire，一种免训练的LLM越狱防御方法。它通过逐神经元假设检验（FDR控制）结合效用特异性筛选，识别安全特异性神经元，然后通过触发器式钳制（trigger-style clamp）将这些神经元固定在有害条件均值激活上，从而注入内部有害输入信号触发对齐期间学到的拒答行为。该方法提供两种可证明等价的部署模式：检测器门控推理时干预和离线偏置补丁权重编辑。在四个安全对齐LLM和四种代表性攻击上，平均攻击成功率降至至多2.0%，MT-Bench效用仅下降0.5%-5.3%，为所有防御方法中最小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经元和路径级别的LLM越狱防御方法存在严重缺陷：一是通过压制毒性神经元来消除有害语义的方法，由于有害语义在网络中分布式存在，阻断所有通路需要巨大的干预足迹；二是基于外部分类器识别安全神经元的方法，常常牺牲对模型效用同样重要的神经元。此外，两类方法始终处于激活状态，即使没有攻击也会扰动所有正常请求，造成不必要的效用损失。

Method: TriPwire首先通过FDR（假发现率）控制的逐神经元假设检验配合效用特异性过滤器来识别安全特异性神经元；然后采用触发器式钳制方法，将所选神经元固定在有害条件均值激活上，注入内部有害输入信号以触发模型对齐阶段学到的拒答行为；最后通过两种可证明等价的部署模式（检测器门控推理时干预和离线偏置补丁权重编辑）实现该钳制。

Result: 在四个安全对齐LLM和四种代表性攻击的实验中，TriPwire将平均攻击成功率降至至多2.0%，MT-Bench效用下降仅为0.5%-5.3%，是所有防御方法中效用损失最小的。

Conclusion: TriPwire通过精准识别安全特异性神经元并采用条件化触发机制，实现了在有效防御越狱攻击的同时最大限度保护模型效用，且为免训练方法，具备实用部署价值。

Abstract: Neuron- and path-level interventions offer the finest-grained route to defending large language models (LLMs) against jailbreak attacks, yet existing methods fall short of this promise, i.e., they often compromise model utility significantly. Specifically, one line of work suppresses toxic neurons to erase harmful semantics, but since such semantics are distributed across the network, blocking every pathway forces a large intervention footprint. An alternative line of research focus on identify safety neurons using external classifiers. While promising, the existing approaches suffer from compromising neurons that are important for the model utility as well. Moreover, both approaches remain always on and thus perturb every benign request even when no attack is present. To address these limitations, we present \ours{}, a training-free defense that first identifies safety-specific neurons through per-neuron hypothesis tests under false-discovery-rate control together with a utility-specificity filter. Based on this identification, a trigger-style clamp holds the selected neurons at their harmful-conditional mean activations, injecting an internal harmful-input signal that triggers the refusal behavior learned during alignment. The clamp is then realized by two provably equivalent deployment modes, namely a detector-gated inference-time intervention and an offline bias-patch weight edit. Extensive experiments across four safety-aligned LLMs and four representative attacks demonstrate that \ours{} reduces the average attack success rate to at most 2.0\% while incurring a utility drop of only 0.5\% to 5.3\% on MT-Bench, the smallest among all defenses. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Tripwire-65C4.

</details>


### [112] [The Past and Future of AI Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14407)
*Ross D. King*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了AI科学家的过去与未来——即能够自动化科学发现的机器系统。从亚当（首个通过假设形成和物理实验循环做出新科学发现的机器）和夏娃（现代自动驾驶实验室架构的建立者）出发，指出基础模型、自主智能体和实验室机器人已使构建远超这两者的通用系统成为可能。核心问题不再是单个组件能否自动化，而是集成问题——AI科学家需将神经学习与逻辑、概率、数学、因果推理、模拟、实验设计、机器人和正式科学记录相结合。AI科学家有潜力使科学更快、更便宜、更系统化、更可复现，并能研究过于复杂的人类难以独立处理的系统。诺贝尔图灵挑战设定了到2050年实现自动化诺贝尔级发现的目标，目前进展超前于计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学自动化已成为可能，但现有工作缺乏对AI科学家这一整体系统概念的系统梳理和对未来方向的整合性视角。作者希望通过综述该领域的过去与未来，阐明核心挑战从组件自动化转向系统集成，并指出AI科学家在加速和变革科学发现方面的巨大潜力。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，回顾了AI科学家的发展历程：从Adam（假设形成与物理实验循环的自动化发现）到Eve（自动驾驶实验室架构的建立），再到当前由基础模型、自主智能体与实验室机器人驱动的更通用系统。文章识别出核心挑战是不同科学能力组件（神经学习、逻辑、概率、数学、因果推理、模拟、实验设计、机器人和正式记录）的集成问题，并参考Nobel Turing Challenge作为未来框架进行展望。

Result: 文章指出组件的自动化已不再是瓶颈，集成为根本挑战；同时表明Nobel Turing Challenge设定的2050年目标进展超前于计划，AI科学家能够提升科研速度、降低成本、增强系统性和可复现性，并能处理人类难以独立研究的复杂系统，支持数千个AI科学家协作解决单一问题。

Conclusion: AI科学家的成功实现将创造一种全新的科学形式并深刻变革世界。该领域的核心任务是解决不同科学推理与实验能力的深度集成问题，而当前的技术基础已使这一目标比预期更早地接近实现。

Abstract: We present a survey of the past and future of AI Scientists: machines capable of automating science. AI Scientists can originate hypotheses, deduce their consequences, design and execute experiments, interpret their results, and revise their beliefs. Such systems are integrated scientific agents, connected to the literature, formal knowledge, mathematical models, simulations, data-analysis systems and physical laboratories.
  Adam was the first machine to make novel scientific discoveries through cycles of hypothesis formation and physical experimentation. Eve established the architecture of the modern self-driving laboratory. Foundation models, autonomous agents and laboratory robotics now make it possible to build systems far more general than either Adam or Eve.
  The central problem is no longer whether individual components of science can be automated. They can. The problem is integration. AI Scientists must combine neural learning with logic, probability, mathematics, causal reasoning, simulation, experimental design, robotics and formal scientific records.
  AI Scientists have the potential to transform science: to make science faster, cheaper, more systematic and more reproducible. AI Scientists could investigate systems too complicated for unaided human science, and enable thousands of AI scientists to work together on single problems.
  The Nobel Turing Challenge sets the goal of developing by 2050 AI systems capable of automating Nobel-quality discoveries. Progress is ahead of schedule. When we succeed it will create a new form of science and transform the world.

</details>


### [113] [Knowing When to Stop: Bayesian Optimal Stopping for LLM Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14425)
*Toby D. Pilditch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出optstop，一个基于精度的自适应停止框架，将LLM评估视为序贯测量问题：在不确定性高的地方继续采样，在估计精确或足够稳定时停止。基于分层贝叶斯推断，支持二值、有序和连续结果，无需校准题库。在200项、10轮的示例评估中，在九种验证设置下可减少57%-97%的计划试验，同时总体结论与完整运行等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估使用固定采样预算，即使估计已经精确，每个测试项也会被评测相同的次数，这造成了计算资源的浪费。作者希望将计算分配从固定的重复次数转变为基于不确定性的动态分配。

Method: 提出optstop框架：(1)基于分层贝叶斯推断构建序贯测量问题；(2)支持二值、有序和连续结果类型；(3)所有基准项保持可采样资格，无需校准题库；(4)支持在线运行或事后（回顾性）运行；(5)包含安全机制：当测量性能趋近于零时更谨慎地采样，因为此时罕见成功至关重要。

Result: 在一个200项、10轮的示例评估中，optstop在九个验证设置下移除了57%-97%的计划试验，且总体结论与完整运行结果等价。

Conclusion: LLM评估的计算量可以根据不确定性而非固定重复次数进行分配，节省的幅度取决于评估设计。这为LLM评估提供了一种更高效的计算资源配置方式。

Abstract: LLM evaluations often use fixed sampling budgets, testing every item the same number of times even after estimates are precise. We introduce optstop, a precision-based adaptive stopping framework that treats evaluation as a sequential measurement problem: keep sampling where uncertainty remains high, and stop where estimates are precise or stable enough. The framework builds on hierarchical Bayesian inference, supports binary, ordinal, and continuous outcomes, and keeps every benchmark item eligible for sampling, without requiring a calibrated item bank. It runs live or retrospectively, and includes a safeguard that samples more cautiously as measured performance approaches zero, where rare successes matter most. In an illustrative 200-item, 10-epoch evaluation, it removes 57%-97% of planned trials across nine validation settings, with overall conclusions equivalent to the full run. These results show that LLM evaluation compute can be allocated by uncertainty rather than by fixed repetition counts, with the magnitude of savings depending on evaluation design.

</details>


### [114] [The Dynamics of Intelligence Explosions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14426)
*Toby Ord*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了AI自我改进（AI用于AI研发）的反馈回路可能引发的智能爆炸现象，通过数学建模分析其动力学特征，指出"奇点式增长"（趋向垂直渐近线）比经济学启发模型所预测的更难实现，并发现一类被忽视的重要增长率——比指数增长更快但不导致垂直渐近线。同时强调"生成时间"（完成一轮反馈回路所需时间）是被忽视但起关键作用的参数，只有生成时间快速趋近零才能实现奇点式增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI越来越多地参与AI研发，这种自我改进的反馈回路在特定条件下可能产生智能爆炸。本文旨在从数学角度理解这种爆炸性过程的驱动力，澄清现有经济学启发式建模中对增长动态的误解，并揭示被忽视的关键参数。

Method: 采用数学分析方法，研究智能爆炸最极端情况下的增长动态，系统分类不同增长率类型（次指数、指数、超指数、奇点式增长），特别分析生成时间参数对系统行为的影响，并与近期经济学启发式模型进行对比。

Result: 研究发现：1) 奇点式增长（趋向垂直渐近线）比近期经济学启发式模型所预测的更难达成；2) 存在一类此前被忽视的增长率——快于指数但不会导致垂直渐近线；3) 生成时间是决定智能爆炸行为走向的关键参数，奇点式增长要求生成时间必须快速趋近于零。

Conclusion: 生成时间作为核心参数应在智能爆炸的建模中得到更多关注。对奇点式增长的实现条件需要更严格的要求，而对超指数但非奇点的增长类别值得更深入的研究，这将有助于更准确地预测AI自我改进的动态路径。

Abstract: AI is increasingly being used to help with AI R&D. Under certain conditions this feedback loop might be able to produce an intelligence explosion, with rapidly escalating AI capabilities. I explore the mathematics of the most explosive possibilities, with an eye to understanding what drives the dynamics. I show that singular growth (towards a vertical asymptote) is harder to achieve than would be expected from recent economics-inspired modelling, and that there is an important but neglected class of growth rates that are faster than exponential but don't lead to a vertical asymptote. I draw out the generation time (the time to go around the feedback loop) as a neglected parameter that plays a pivotal role in determining the behaviour of any intelligence explosion --- one cannot have singular growth unless the generation time rapidly approaches zero.

</details>


### [115] [PACE-Bench: Benchmarking Physics Adaptation via Code Evolution in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14441)
*Yuhao Zhan,Bingxiang He,Zecong Tang,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PACE-Bench，一个基于物理模拟器的自进化智能体评估基准，包含144个源到目标环境适应对，横跨六个物理域。评估发现现有自进化方法在该基准上远未饱和——表现最好的方法仅解决35.9%的任务，同时揭示基于模拟器反馈的反思机制比未验证的自我修订更可靠，机制重新设计而非参数推断是核心瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自进化智能体评估通常在固定执行条件下进行，未测试环境条件变化后智能体的恢复能力。为了填补这一空白，作者提出了PACE-Bench基准，专门评估智能体在物理环境突变下的适应能力。

Method: PACE-Bench构建了144个源到目标环境适应对，覆盖六个物理域。每个对应关系将源环境与一个突变的目标环境连接，保持相同目标和接口。在源环境中成功的代码驱动设计会在目标环境中失败，智能体需要利用诊断沙箱反馈，在有限的尝试预算内迭代地将源设计适应为目标环境的工作设计。论文比较了来自四种范式的十种自进化方法。

Result: 基准远未饱和：Reflexion + Qwen3-14B仅解决35.9%的全基准对，而GPT-5.5在完整预算下解决了Statics子集的66.7%。结果显示基于模拟器grounded的反思比未验证的自我修订更可靠，记忆机制将智能体锚定在早期设计上，而大范围树搜索探索但无法收敛。即使揭示确切的物理变化也没有提高性能上限。

Conclusion: 模拟器grounded的反思是自进化智能体适应环境变化的关键，而主要的瓶颈在于机制重新设计而非参数推断。该基准为未来自进化智能体的研究提供了具有挑战性的评估平台。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents improve future behavior from interaction experience, yet existing evaluations typically optimize under fixed execution conditions and do not test recovery after those conditions change. To address this gap, we introduce PACE-Bench (Physics Adaptation via Code Evolution), a simulator-grounded benchmark of 144 source-to-target adaptation pairs across six physics domains. Each pair links a source environment to a mutated target environment with the same goal and interface. A code-driven design that succeeds in the source fails in the target, where agents must iteratively adapt it into a working target design using diagnostic sandbox feedback within a limited attempt budget. We compare ten self-evolving methods from four paradigms. The benchmark remains far from saturated: Reflexion + Qwen3-14B succeeds on only 35.9\% of full-benchmark pairs, while GPT-5.5 solves 66.7\% of the Statics subset under the full budget. Together, these results show that simulator-grounded reflection is more reliable than unverified self-revision, while memory anchors agents to early designs and broad tree search explores without converging. Even revealing exact physical changes does not raise the performance ceiling, pointing to mechanism redesign rather than parameter inference as the central bottleneck. Data and code are available at https://github.com/thunlp/PACE-Bench.

</details>


### [116] [Wyvern: An Agentic Framework for Generating Grounded Multimodal Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14446)
*Beatrice Alessandra Motetti,Emilien Guandalino,Daniele Jahier Pagliari,Alessio Burrello,Lorenz K. Müller,Konstantin Berestizshevsky,Lukas Cavigelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Wyvern，一个用于自动生成有依据（grounded）、多模态技术报告的多智能体框架。该框架集成图像、表格和文本并附带引用，并通过声明自动修订阶段来保证内容的grounding。人工评估显示其图表信息性在87%案例中优于基线，报告有用性在63%-100%的实例中超过三种替代方法；自动评估中引用召回率提升2.3倍，引用精确率提升1.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI驱动的创新时代，知识增长加速，人类难以跟上。虽然生成模型越来越多地用于内容合成，但它们往往缺乏信息grounding（信息依据）。为了解决这些时代特征，需要一种能生成既多模态又有充分依据的技术报告的框架。

Method: Wyvern是一个多智能体框架，能够生成多模态输出，将图像、表格和文本与支持性引用整合在统一报告中。框架特别强调内容的grounding，实现了声明的自动修订（claims auto-revision）阶段。评价方法包括人工评估和自动评估，与多种基线方法进行对比。

Result: 人工评估表明：Wyvern生成的图表信息性在87%的案例中被认为优于近期基线；报告的有用性在63%-100%的实例中高于三种替代方法。自动评估显示：与前文基线相比，Wyvern的引用召回率最高提升2.3倍，引用精确率最高提升1.6倍。

Conclusion: Wyvern通过多智能体协作和声明自动修订机制，在生成多模态技术报告的同时有效提升了内容的grounding质量，在信息性、有用性和引用准确性方面均显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: In the current artificial intelligence-driven innovation era, the pace of knowledge growth is accelerating, and is hard to keep up with. While generative models are increasingly used to synthesize content, they often lack in information grounding. To address these peculiarities of our time, we propose Wyvern, a multi-agent framework for the automated generation of grounded, multimodal technical reports. Wyvern allows for the generation of multimodal outputs, integrating images, tables, and text with supporting references in a unified report. Additionally, a particular focus is placed on the grounding of the content, with the implementation of a claims auto-revision stage. We conduct a human evaluation study to assess the quality of our proposed framework. The results show that the figures' informativeness is perceived as superior to that of a recent baseline in 87% of cases. Furthermore, Wyvern's reports are rated as more useful than those produced by three alternative methods in 63% to 100% of instances. We also carry out automatic evaluations showing that Wyvern gains up to 2.3$\times$ in citation recall and 1.6$\times$ in citation precision with respect to the baselines.

</details>


### [117] [SheetCompass: Hierarchical Relation Graphs for Agentic Spreadsheet Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14452)
*Panjing He,Mingyue Cheng,Yucong Luo,Li Li,Xiaohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出SheetCompass，一个基于图和记忆驱动的智能体框架，用于解决LLM在电子表格推理中的挑战。该框架显式建模工作表内外的结构关系，并在记忆中维护任务相关信息，使智能体能够在复杂工作簿上更有效地推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管电子表格被广泛使用，但LLM在自动化电子表格推理方面仍面临挑战。现实中的工作簿常包含隐式跨表关联、细粒度列依赖和复杂空间布局。现有方法通常将多维结构扁平化为顺序字符串，丢失了工作表内部边界和跨工作表语义，导致LLM无法利用人类专家在检查电子表格时自然依赖的全局空间上下文。

Method: SheetCompass是一个图引导和记忆驱动的agentic框架，它显式建模工作表内（intra-sheet）和跨工作表（inter-sheet）的结构关系，同时在记忆中维护任务相关信息，使智能体能够更有效地对复杂工作簿进行推理和自动化操作。

Result: 摘要未提供具体的实验结果数据，但框架设计旨在提升LLM在复杂电子表格工作簿上的推理与自动化能力。

Conclusion: SheetCompass通过显式建模电子表格的结构化关系并引入记忆机制，克服了现有扁平化方法丢失空间语义的局限，为LLM驱动的电子表格推理提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Spreadsheets are widely used to organize, analyze, and manipulate semi-structured data, yet automated spreadsheet reasoning remains challenging for large language models (LLMs). Real-world workbooks often contain implicit cross-table associations, fine-grained column dependencies, and complex spatial layouts. Existing methods typically flatten these multidimensional structures into sequential strings, losing important intra-sheet boundaries and inter-sheet semantics. Consequently, LLMs cannot exploit the global spatial context that human experts naturally use when inspecting spreadsheets. We propose SheetCompass, a graph-guided and memory-driven agentic framework for spreadsheet reasoning and automation. SheetCompass explicitly models structural relationships within and across worksheets while maintaining task-relevant information in memory, enabling agents to reason more effectively over complex workbooks.

</details>


### [118] [Shift Aware Transfer Learning with Adaptive Dual-Encoder Fusion for PM Forecasting in Data-Limited Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14456)
*Shahab Band,Hamed Mohammadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种位移感知双编码器迁移框架，用于短时程PM2.5预报。该框架结合源域（美国10个监测站点）预训练知识与目标域（台湾77个站点）特定表示学习，在冻源头编码器设置下取得MSE=21.8960、MAE=3.1597、R²=0.8725的最佳基线性能，相较于直接迁移学习降低了约7.1%（TL-v1）和4.1%（TL-v2）的MSE。消融实验表明台湾特定分支对性能贡献最大；允许源编码器在目标监督下自适应，获得了最优整体结果（MSE=21.6575）。SHAP分析显示预测主要由近期PM2.5观测及与污染物传输和扩散相关的气象变量驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在目标域观测数据有限且源域与目标域统计特性不同的情况下，短时程PM2.5预报仍然困难。仅使用本地数据训练的模型可能无法捕捉复杂的时间动态，而直接迁移学习可能导致负迁移。因此需要一个能够同时利用源域知识并保留目标域特定信息的框架。

Method: 构建了一个位移感知双编码器迁移框架：源编码器使用美国10个监测站点的小时观测数据进行预训练；随后采用时间顺序的训练-验证-测试协议，在台湾77个站点两年的小时观测数据上进行适配和评估。框架包含双编码器结构——一个承载源域知识（可冻结或自适应），一个针对台湾目标域特定表示学习的分支，并进行了消融分析验证各组件的贡献。

Result: 在四个主要基线中，冻源头双编码器模型表现最佳：MSE=21.8960，MAE=3.1597，R²=0.8725，相较于TL-v1降低约7.1%的MSE，相较于TL-v2降低约4.1%。消融分析显示移除台湾特定分支导致最大性能下降。允许源编码器在目标监督下自适应获得最佳整体结果：MSE=21.6575，MAE=3.1383，R²=0.8739。SHAP分析表明预测主要依赖近期PM2.5观测值及与污染物传输和扩散相关的气象变量。

Conclusion: 源域知识在最有效时需满足两个条件：保留目标域特定信息（台湾特定分支必不可少），且允许迁移表示在目标监督下自适应调整（而非完全冻结）。该框架有效缓解了负迁移问题，为跨域PM2.5预报提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Short-horizon forecasting of fine particulate matter (PM2.5) remains difficult when observations from the target domain are limited and the statistical properties of the source and target domains differ. In these settings, models trained only on local data may not capture complex temporal dynamics, while direct transfer learning can result in negative transfer. This study develops a shift-aware dual-encoder transfer framework that combines source-domain knowledge with target-specific representation learning. The source encoder was pretrained using hourly observations from 10 U.S. monitoring locations. The framework was then adapted and evaluated using two years of hourly observations from 77 stations in Taiwan under a chronological train-validation-test protocol. Among the four principal baselines, the frozen-source dual-encoder model achieved the best performance, with MSE = 21.8960, MAE = 3.1597, and R^2 = 0.8725. This corresponds to an MSE reduction of approximately 7.1% relative to TL-v1 and 4.1% relative to TL-v2. The ablation analysis showed that removing the Taiwan-specific branch caused the largest decline in performance. Allowing the source encoder to adapt produced the best overall result, with MSE = 21.6575, MAE = 3.1383, and R^2 = 0.8739. SHAP analysis indicated that predictions were driven mainly by recent PM2.5 observations and meteorological variables related to pollutant transport and dispersion. These results suggest that source-domain knowledge is most effective when target-specific information is preserved and the transferred representation is allowed to adapt under target supervision.

</details>


### [119] [Twin: Playing an Unknown Game with a Test-Time Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14490)
*Alexy Skoutnev,Kirill Acharya,Gaston Longhitano,Madeleine Udell,Kevin Ellis,Iddo Drori*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Test-time World-model Inference (Twin)系统，让前沿编码智能体通过模拟和交互自动编写可执行的世界模型，在ARC-AGI-3基准上清除97.8%的关卡，且效率优于88.3%的人类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法需要为每个任务手工设计世界模型，无法应对隐藏规则和目标的持续学习任务（如ARC-AGI-3游戏）。目标是构建一个能够仅通过模拟和交互来自主推断游戏规则和目标的系统。

Method: Twin系统让一个前沿编码智能体编写可执行的世界模型。系统对网格游戏有足够强的归纳先验，能够恢复游戏的真实转移函数和目标。采用"孪生世界模型"进行回放验证，强制要求动作在程序复现所有先前的观察转移之前不得执行。每个预测与实际动作结果之间的不匹配都作为反例用于修复世界模型。

Result: Twin在183个关卡中清除179个（97.8%），其中158个（88.3%）比人类更高效。87.2%的关卡在获得任何奖励之前就推断出目标，其余关卡通过搜索自动发现。基础模型直接得分仅7.8%，现成harness提升至61.1%，而Twin将同一基础模型提升至93.3%。

Conclusion: 构建可用世界模型比预想的简单，而真正困难的问题在于推断正确的目标。世界模型与目标推断的分离是持续学习任务中的关键洞察。

Abstract: We present a Test-time World-model Inference (Twin) system, in which a frontier coding agent writes an executable world model for completing continual learning tasks, such as ARC-AGI-3 games. Traditional approaches hand-engineer such models, one custom design per task. Each game hides its rules and goal, and our system constructs them from simulation and interaction alone. Its inductive prior over grid games is strong enough to recover the true transitions of the game and the goal on nearly all levels. Replay validation happens in a twin world model. The harness enforces that an action is not made until the program reproduces every previous observed game transition. Each mismatch between a world model prediction and the actual action result becomes a counterexample that is used to repair the world model. Twin clears 179 out of 183 levels (97.8%), and does so more efficiently than humans in 158 out of 179 levels (88.3%). The system infers the goal before any reward on 156 of the levels it clears (87.2%), and in the remaining levels automatically discovers the goal by search. The benchmark scores completion and action efficiency, between 0 and 100, against humans playing each game for the first time. Played directly, the base model scores only 7.8%; an off-the-shelf harness increases it to 61.1%, whereas our twin world model increases the same base model to 93.3%, clearing 23 out of 25 games. Building a usable world model is simpler than anticipated, whereas the harder problem is inferring the right goal.

</details>


### [120] [Participatory Moral AI Is Not Neutral: The Invisible Hand of Developers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14522)
*Taenyun Kim,Edyta Bogucka,Daniele Quercia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过两个阶段（N=809）的实验研究，系统考察了道德AI偏好提取流水线中三个关键决策——特征范围界定、投票者抽样和问题框架——如何影响AI系统的道德偏好结果，发现这三个看似"技术性"的选择实际上都具有规范性影响，主张道德AI对齐不能仅靠聚合投票实现，各环节应被审计和披露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统在社会中做出越来越多的道德决策，研究者采用道德偏好提取方法——让参与者对假设性困境投票，聚合结果训练AI策略。然而在投票前，开发者需要在流水线中做三个关键选择（特征范围、投票者抽样、问题框架），这些选择通常不透明、未记录，被当作技术细节而非规范性决策处理。作者旨在揭示这些"技术细节"实际上如何系统性地塑造道德偏好结果。

Method: 研究采用两阶段实证设计（N=809），在三个部署场景（AI肾脏分配、AI模拟缺席员工、生成式AI再现逝者）中检验道德AI提取流水线的三个主要阶段：1）比较不同场景中道德相关特征的转移性；2）分析政治意识形态对偏好差异的影响（约占三分之一的特征表现出差异，部分方向反转）；3）操控提问措辞以观察其如何扩大或缩小意识形态差距。

Result: 研究发现：1）道德相关特征在不同部署场景间发生转移，特征模式不应假设可以跨领域复用；2）约三分之一的特征上，偏好因政治意识形态而异，且部分差异方向相反，投票者池的意识形态构成会影响聚合偏好概况；3）问题措辞可将意识形态差距改变多达一个完整量表点，框架条件还会改变道德基础与参与者判断的关联方式。

Conclusion: 投票式对齐无法仅靠聚合就实现公平或透明的AI。道德AI提取流水线的每个阶段（特征范围界定、投票者抽样、问题框架）至少都应被审计和公开披露，因为这些看似中立的"技术选择"实质上承载着规范性含义。

Abstract: As AI systems make more morally loaded decisions across society, one response has been moral preference elicitation. In this approach, researchers poll participants on hypothetical dilemmas and use the aggregated votes to train a policy that an AI model then applies at scale. Before any vote is cast, developers make three key choices in the moral AI elicitation pipeline: feature scoping, voter sampling, and question framing. In other words, they decide which features go to a vote, which voters to include, and how to present the question. These choices are often opaque, undocumented, and treated as technical details rather than normative ones. We examine each of these choices within a common empirical study and show that each can shape the preferences produced by moral AI elicitation. Across two phases (N = 809) in three deployment contexts (i.e., AI kidney allocation, AI agents simulating absent workers, and generative AI depictions of the deceased), we examine the three main stages of the moral AI elicitation pipeline. First, morally relevant features shift across contexts. This suggests that feature schemas should not be assumed to transfer across deployment domains. Second, preferences differ by political ideology for roughly one-third of features, with some differences reversing direction. The ideological composition of the voter pool can therefore affect the resulting aggregated preference profile. Third, the wording of the elicitation question can narrow or widen ideological gaps by up to a full scale point. The framing conditions also change how moral foundations are associated with participants' judgments. Taken together, these findings suggest that voting-based alignment cannot deliver fair or transparent AI by aggregation alone; at minimum, each stage of the moral AI elicitation pipeline should be audited and disclosed.

</details>


### [121] [Handover of In-Context Learning State Across Session Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14528)
*Masahiro Kato,Taka Kato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型应用中会话交接（session handover）的方法论与理论性质，将会话交接形式化为任务相关的上下文学习（ICL）状态的转移，区分了早期内容的精确恢复与目标分布的保持，并在外生性条件下用预测等价性刻画了最粗的确定性充分交接及其定长比特需求。文章提出了一个三部分记录方案，并在高斯线性回归和非参数回归中给出了具体的结果与记忆-误差权衡界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM应用中，当上下文达到模型输入限制、应用重启或切换代理继续任务时，任务需要在新的会话中继续，但必须决定从先前会话传递哪些信息。该问题缺乏系统的理论和方法的刻画：什么信息必须保留、记忆需求如何依赖于后续任务，这些问题尚无严谨的答案。

Method: 将会话交接形式化为任务相关ICL状态的转移，区分精确恢复与分布保持；在外生性条件下，利用预测等价性刻画最粗的确定性充分交接并推导固定长度比特需求；提出三部分记录结构：存储决策与约束的精确部分、使用任务合理化统计量处理重复证据的部分、保留未被统计量压制的原始观测的部分；在参数（高斯线性回归）与非参数（非参数回归）两大框架下分别给出精确有限维交接、有限比特扰动界以及记忆与平方预测误差之间的上下界。

Result: 分析隔离了记忆约束、写入者和续接程序各自的影响，量化了在已知下游查询之前写入的成本。高斯线性回归给出了精确的有限维握手和有限比特扰动界；非参数回归给出了将记忆与平方预测误差相联系的上界和下界。

Conclusion: 这项工作为会话交接的内容选择和记忆需求的量化提供了理论框架与方法，明确了什么信息必须被保留以及其记忆开销如何随续接任务而变化。

Abstract: This study investigates the methodological and theoretical properties of session handover in applications that use large language models. A task may continue in a new session when the context reaches the model's input limit, when the application restarts, or when another agent is asked to finish the task. The application must then decide which information from the earlier session to pass on. We formulate handover as the transfer of a task-relative in-context learning (ICL) state and distinguish exact recovery of earlier material from preservation of the target distribution. Under an exogeneity condition, predictive equivalence characterizes the coarsest deterministic sufficient handover and gives a fixed-length bit requirement. The analysis isolates the effects of the memory constraint, the writer, and the continuation procedure, and quantifies the cost of writing before the realized downstream query is known. We propose a three-part record that stores decisions and constraints exactly, uses task-justified statistics for repeated evidence, and retains original observations whose effect is not preserved by those statistics. Gaussian linear regression gives an exact finite-dimensional handover and finite-bit perturbation bounds, while nonparametric regression gives upper and lower bounds that relate memory to squared prediction error. These results provide a theory and method for deciding what a handover must retain and how its memory requirement depends on the continuation task.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] ["I Thought You Were The Uncensored Place": Norms, Rules, and Moderation in AI-Generated Sexual Content Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13659)
*Lucy Qin,Jaron Mink,Elissa M. Redmiles*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究通过访谈24名大型AI生成性内容（AIG-SC）在线社区的成员和版主，探讨社区治理如何在保护言论自由的同时防止虐待性内容的生产，发现反审查和非评判的文化规范与内容审核之间存在张力，为虐待行为留下空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI生成性内容（AIG-SC）生产日益增多，在线社区应运而生以支持创作者需求。但目前缺乏对这些社区治理机制如何运作、能否在防止虐待性内容（如AI生成的CSAM）与支持自由表达之间取得平衡的深入理解。

Method: 对24名来自10,000人以上大型AIG-SC在线社区且明确禁止创建和分享虐待性内容（如AI生成的CSAM）的成员和版主进行深入访谈，分析其社区形成动因、隐性规范、明文规则及其通过内容审核的运作方式，以及社区价值观与审核之间的张力。

Result: 研究发现：（1）尽管许多创作者和社区对虐待性内容设有个人边界，但创建任何形式AI生成性内容的建议和资源均对所有用户开放，不受其意图限制；（2）社区中反审查和非评判的规范使版主只能依据法律和平台服务条款来正当化其审核行为，与社区价值观产生张力，为虐待性行为留下空间。

Conclusion: 技术、社区和法律三种治理机制需要协同作用才能最有效地减少虐待性内容的生产，需要重新审视现行审核策略的局限并探索更有效的治理方案。

Abstract: As AI-generated sexual content (AIG-SC) is increasingly produced, online communities have emerged to support creators' needs. To understand whether and how community governance attempts work to prevent abuse while supporting free expression, we interviewed 24 members and moderators of large AIG-SC online communities (10,000+ members) with stated rules against creating and sharing abusive content (e.g., AI-generated CSAM). Through in-depth interviews, we offer insight into: (1) how and why these communities form; (2) implicit community norms; (3) explicitly stated rules---and their operationalization via content moderation; and (4) tensions between community values and moderation that leave space for abusive behavior.
  Our findings reveal a complex picture: while many creators and communities have personal boundaries against abuse, advice and resources for creating any form of AI-generated sexual content are accessible to users regardless of their intentions. Further complicating community moderation are norms that center anti-censorship and non-judgment, which leave moderators to justify their actions using the limits of the law and terms of service. We end by reflecting on the ways in which technical, community, and legal governance may most effectively mitigate the production of abusive content.

</details>


### [123] [Weird Machines in Transport Layer Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13685)
*Michael Collins,Jada Cumberland,Brianne Dunn,Ross Gore,Samuel Jackson,Sachin Shetty,Jonathan Takeshita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文将怪癖机器（weird machines）理论扩展到TLS握手协议领域，证明OpenSSL和BoringSSL等实现中的合法TLS原语（会话缓存、重协商逻辑、扩展解析、证书验证）可以组合成图灵完备系统，并提出了"信任驱动"（trust actuation）这一新概念，通过两个真实演示验证了该理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有怪癖机器研究主要集中于软件系统（x86指令、ELF元数据、页表）和网络物理系统（工业控制网络），但尚未系统性地研究安全协议领域中的潜在计算能力。作者希望将这一理论扩展到TLS握手协议，揭示安全协议中隐藏的计算可能性及其对认证和信任决策的影响。

Method: 作者首先形式化分析了TLS握手协议中可用原语的组合能力，提出了"信任驱动"概念，论证了拥有会话存储、序列计数器算术、握手状态条件分支和恢复/重试循环迭代的实现即可构成任意计算。随后基于真实OpenSSL代码路径构建了两个演示系统：一个哨兵（sentinel）防御机制和一个人认证绕过攻击，二者均在Docker中针对真实服务器和客户端二进制运行验证。

Result: 两个工作演示均成功：哨兵系统能够通过组合标准TLS原语检测异常握手行为；认证绕过攻击通过中连接重协商（mid-connection renegotiation）成功击败了密码强度策略检查，且无需内存损坏或外部恶意软件。这证明TLS合法原语确实可以组合为图灵完备的计算系统，其计算能力与认证和信任决策耦合。

Conclusion: TLS握手协议及其主流实现（OpenSSL、BoringSSL）中存在可通过合法原语组合形成的图灵完备"怪癖机器"，这种计算能力与信任决策而非物理驱动相耦合，构成了一个全新的安全研究领域。这提示安全社区需要重新审视即使合规的TLS实现也可能被组合利用为计算资源的安全风险。

Abstract: Weird machines are latent computational capabilities that emerge from the composition of architectural components. Prior work has studied this phenomenon extensively in software systems, including x86 instructions, ELF metadata, and page tables, and more recently in cyber-physical systems such as industrial control networks. This paper extends weird machine theory to a new domain: the Transport Layer Security (TLS) handshake and its two dominant implementations, OpenSSL and BoringSSL.
  We show that legitimate TLS primitives, including session cache entries, renegotiation logic, extension parsing, and certificate verification steps, compose into Turing-complete systems whose computation is coupled to authentication and trust decisions rather than physical actuation. We formalize this coupling, which we call trust actuation, and argue that any TLS implementation providing session storage, arithmetic on sequence counters, conditional branching on handshake state, and iteration through resumption or retry loops satisfies the conditions for arbitrary computation.
  We validate this theory with two working demonstrations built on real OpenSSL code paths. The first, a sentinel system, composes standard TLS primitives into a defensive mechanism that detects anomalous handshake behavior. The second, an authentication bypass, composes the same class of primitives into an attack that defeats a cipher-strength policy check through mid-connection renegotiation, without any memory corruption or external malware. Both demonstrations run against real server and client binaries in Docker.

</details>


### [124] [A Reproducibility Protocol for Cross-Implementation Evaluation of Post-Quantum ACVP Test Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13784)
*Christopher M. Frost*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文定义了一个产品中立的ML-KEM可复现性协议（Protocol v2），对三个公开实现（@noble/post-quantum 0.7.0、liboqs 0.16.0、Go 1.26.4）在固定NIST ACVP语料上进行了2160条基础记录的交叉验证，全部1650条可执行评估与NIST预言机一致，510条不支持记录与Go能力边界一致，实现了完整的成对一致性，无任何失败或错误，证明了作者可复现性并揭示了公共ML-KEM包在确定性和验证测试表面上的实质性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码学标准的不同实现应产生相同的已知答案结果，但只有当语料、修订版本、公共接口、排除项和证据都被精确说明时，一致性才具有意义。现有公共ML-KEM实现缺乏统一的、产品中立的可复现性验证协议。

Method: 定义Protocol v2：冻结供应商特定能力、对称应用统一验证错误分类法、保留所有选定用例、分离字节/验证结论/不支持操作/适配器错误。基于源码构建三个实现，在三轮重复中执行完整的笛卡尔积验证，包括2160条基础记录以及密钥生成投影诊断和三个冻结对照实验。

Result: 1650条声明可执行的评估全部与NIST预言机匹配，510条不支持记录全部匹配Go预声明能力边界；成对重叠上完全一致（noble-liboqs 720/720，各Go配对210/210）；150条Go封装密钥投影全部匹配；三个冻结对照均产生精确的预声明结果；无基础失败、适配器错误或状态不稳定。

Conclusion: 研究确立了有界的作者可复现性，同时暴露了实际标准缺口：公共ML-KEM包提供实质性不同的确定性和验证测试表面。独立外部复现尚未被观察到，且结果不构成认证、穷举正确性、侧信道抗性、安全集成或生产保证。

Abstract: Independent implementations of a cryptographic standard should reproduce the same known-answer results, yet agreement is meaningful only when the corpus, revisions, public interfaces, exclusions, and evidence are precisely stated. This study defines a product-neutral reproducibility protocol for three public implementations of NIST ML-KEM against a pinned public Automated Cryptographic Validation Protocol corpus. Protocol v2 freezes provider-specific capabilities, applies one validation-error taxonomy symmetrically, preserves every selected case, and separates bytes, validation verdicts, unsupported operations, and adapter errors. The source-built experiment evaluated @noble/post-quantum 0.7.0, liboqs 0.16.0, and Go 1.26.4. Across three repetitions, the required Cartesian product comprised 2,160 base records: all 1,650 declared executable evaluations matched the NIST oracle, and all 510 unsupported records matched Go's predeclared capability boundary. Pairwise agreement was complete on every executable overlap: 720 of 720 noble-liboqs records and 210 of 210 records for each Go pairing. A separate keyGen-ek-projection diagnostic matched all 150 Go encapsulation-key projections without counting them as full key generation. Three frozen controls independently exercised byte comparison, verdict comparison, and malformed-response error separation; each produced its exact predeclared outcome. No base failure, adapter error, or status instability occurred. The evidence establishes bounded author-run repeatability and exposes a practical standards gap: public ML-KEM packages provide materially different deterministic and validation-test surfaces. Independent external reproduction remains unobserved. The results do not establish certification, exhaustive correctness, side-channel resistance, secure integration, or production assurance.

</details>


### [125] [The ack3 H1 2026 DeFi Incident Dataset: Audit Scope Across 135 Security Incidents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13792)
*Josef Gattermayer,Jan Kalivoda,Arman Bašović*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 基于2026年H1 DeFi事件数据集，对135起DeFi安全事件进行分析，发现68起有审计记录的事件中，67.6%的攻击路径（按损失计94.4%）落在所有公开审计范围之外，表明项目级审计历史与事件路径审计范围是两个不同的变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约审计通常只覆盖特定时间点的特定工件，但"已审计"标签常被误认为项目级保障。作者希望区分"项目有无审计记录"与"具体攻击路径是否在审计范围内"这两个概念，以评估审计的实际覆盖效果。

Method: 使用网络安全公司ack3发布的H1 2026 DeFi事件数据集（2026年1月1日至6月29日，损失9.3986亿美元），对135起事件进行审计历史和事件路径范围分析。对68起有审计记录的事件，逐一核对攻击路径是否落在所有公开的、事件前审计的范围之内。

Result: 68起有审计记录的事件中，46起（67.6%）攻击路径在所有公开审计范围之外，20起在至少一个范围内，2起未判定。按损失计，范围外路径占94.4%，但该权重集中于两起大型事件，剔除后占比降至72.1%，方向性结论仍成立。

Conclusion: 项目级审计历史与事件路径审计范围是相互独立的不同变量。审计的存在与否不能作为项目安全性的项目级保证，评估审计有效性的关键在于攻击路径是否落入审计覆盖范围。

Abstract: Smart-contract audits cover defined artifacts at a specific time, but the label audited is often treated as project-wide assurance. We analyze audit history and incident-path scope across 135 DeFi security incidents using the H1 2026 DeFi Incident Dataset published by cybersecurity company ack3 (https://ack3.ai), covering 1 January to 29 June 2026. The corpus reports USD 939.86 million in attributed loss. Audit history was identified for 68 incidents. Of these, 46 attack paths were outside all identified public pre-incident audit scopes, 20 were inside at least one scope, and 2 were unresolved. Within this 68-incident subset, outside-scope paths represented 67.6% by count and 94.4% of reported loss. The loss-weighted result was concentrated in two large incidents; excluding both reduced the share to 72.1%, while preserving the direction of the result. We also describe audit age, temporal loss distribution, and affected project types. The results show that project-level audit history and incident-path scope are distinct variables.

</details>


### [126] [Mobile Apps vs. Web Browsers: A User Perception Study with Android Apps and Google Chrome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13803)
*Harel Berger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验和问卷调查，探讨了Android智能手机用户对移动应用与网页浏览器在安全、隐私和可用性方面的感知差异，旨在揭示影响用户平台选择的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户在使用在线服务时面临在移动应用和网页浏览器之间进行选择的情况，而这两种平台在安全、隐私和可用性方面存在差异。了解用户的感知和担忧对于开发者改善两类平台的体验至关重要。

Method: 采用实验与问卷调查相结合的方法，以Android智能手机用户为研究对象，收集用户对移动应用与网页浏览器在安全、隐私和可用性方面的感知数据。

Result: 研究识别出影响用户选择移动应用或网页浏览器的关键关注点和偏好，并揭示了用户存在的相关误解。

Conclusion: 研究结果为开发者提供了有价值的见解，帮助其通过解决用户的担忧和纠正误解，来改进移动应用和网页浏览器在安全、隐私及可用性方面的表现。

Abstract: This study examines user perceptions of mobile applications (apps) versus web browsers for accessing online services, with an emphasis on security, privacy, and usability aspects. Through a combination of an experiment and a survey with Android smartphone users, the research seeks to identify the key concerns and preferences that influence their choice between mobile apps and web browsers. The findings will offer valuable insights for developers to improve the security, privacy, and usability of both platforms by addressing user concerns and misconceptions.

</details>


### [127] [Vaulted Passkeys: A Device-Bound Proposal for Authenticated Credential Export and Import](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13806)
*Pol Henarejos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Vaulted Passkeys架构，通过256位随机密钥保护和恢复硬件认证器中的私有凭据状态，在避免克隆完整认证器且不暴露明文私钥的前提下实现凭据的可移植备份与恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件认证器刻意抵抗私钥提取，但设备更换、灾难恢复和受控迁移存在合法的可移植性需求。现有的设备绑定凭据指南通常通过提前注册额外认证器来降低恢复风险，这实际上是冗余而非原始凭据的备份，且要求备用硬件事先存在，无法真正解决恢复缺口。

Method: 提出Vaulted Passkeys设备绑定架构：使用随机256位Kvault密钥，通过HKDF分离密钥和四种显式AEAD配置文件保护认证的PKV1凭据信封。设计将注册与导出/导入分离，强制性vault与可选身份分离，并贡献了角色分离系统模型、线格式、威胁分析、实现映射和可证伪评估计划。

Result: 原型验证了可行性，但明确说明该工作既不是正式安全证明，也未提出最终标准草案。

Conclusion: Vaulted Passkeys为硬件认证器凭据恢复提供了在源设备可用时导出受保护凭据状态、并恢复到后续获取的硬件中的方案，填补了现有冗余注册机制无法覆盖的恢复缺口的初步尝试。

Abstract: Hardware authenticators deliberately resist private-key extraction, yet replacement, disaster recovery, and controlled migration create a legitimate need for portability. Existing guidance for device-bound credentials commonly reduces recovery risk by registering an additional authenticator before failure. That creates an independent credential registration and requires replacement hardware to exist in advance; it is redundancy, not a backup of the original credential. This paper addresses the resulting recovery gap by exporting protected credential state while the source is available and restoring it to hardware acquired later, without cloning a complete authenticator or exposing plaintext private keys to routine desktop software. We propose Vaulted Passkeys, a device-bound architecture in which a random 256-bit Kvault protects authenticated PKV1 credential envelopes through HKDF-separated keys and four explicit AEAD profiles. The design separates enrollment from export/import and the required vault from optional identity. We contribute a role-separated system model, wire format, threat analysis, implementation mapping, and falsifiable evaluation plan. The prototype demonstrates feasibility but is neither a formal security proof nor a proposed final standard.

</details>


### [128] [TLF: Rapid Characterization of RF Transceiver Parameters in Embedded Systems via Bus-Level Interception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13815)
*Larry Hernandez,Sergey Bratus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出TLF（收发器提升框架），一种从微控制器与收发器IC之间的总线级迹线恢复RF收发器配置和运行时行为的工具。通过有状态协议解码器重建RF参数和键材料，在SX1233和SX1276目标上验证，可单次捕获秒级处理恢复完整RF配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以从硬件层恢复RF收发器的完整配置和运行时行为，特别是在缺乏固件先验知识的情况下。基于总线的RF收发器广泛使用，其在总线上的寄存器写入和FIFO传输包含关键的RF参数和加密密钥信息，需要一种工具来恢复这些信息以配置匹配接收器或开发针对性对策。

Method: TLF包括：1）有状态协议解码器，基于收发器寄存器与数据接口构建；2）拦截并重构寄存器写入与FIFO传输；3）提取RF操作参数和芯片级行为；4）恢复通过主机接口加载的硬件加密密钥；5）从固件驱动的跳频中提取信道表、跳频序列和时序；6）可插拔协议解码器将FIFO负载解释为上层应用PDU（在Meshtastic上端到端演示）。

Result: 在两个目标上评估：基于SX1233的UAV C2调制解调器（固件级FHSS，每包同步字轮换）和基于SX1276的Meshtastic节点（LoRa寄存器覆盖）。单次总线捕获在数秒内恢复完整寄存器暴露的RF配置（调制、频段计划、相位行为），无需固件先验知识，足以配置匹配接收器或开发针对性对策。

Conclusion: TLF能够从总线级迹线提取丰富的RF收发器配置和行为信息，恢复深度受限于收发器寄存器接口：完全在固件中实现的参数（自定义FEC、白化、加密）只能作为不透明的FIFO负载观测。该方法需要物理访问或硬件仿真，固件级加密状态保持不透明。

Abstract: We present TLF (Transceiver Lifter Framework), a tool for recovering RF transceiver configuration and runtime behavior from bus-level traces captured between a microcontroller and its transceiver IC. A stateful protocol decoder, built against the transceiver's register and data interface, reconstructs operating RF parameters and behavior from intercepted register writes and FIFO transfers. For bus-attached transceivers whose hardware-cryptography keys are loaded through the intercepted host interface, key material is also recoverable. Where the firmware drives frequency hopping -- either through a hardware-assisted engine or a custom schedule -- the decoder extracts the channel table, hop sequence, and timing. We evaluate the approach on two targets from different Semtech families: an SX1233-based UAV C2 modem employing firmware-level FHSS with per-packet sync word rotation, and an SX1276-based Meshtastic node exercising the LoRa register overlay. From a single bus capture, processed in seconds, TLF recovers the complete register-exposed RF configuration (modulation, band plan, phase behavior) without prior knowledge of the target firmware -- sufficient to configure a matched receiver or develop targeted countermeasures. Above the chip layer, a pluggable protocol decoder interprets recovered FIFO payloads as application PDUs, demonstrated end-to-end on Meshtastic. Firmware-level cryptographic state remains, as expected, opaque. The approach requires physical access or emulation of the target hardware, and its recovery depth is bounded by the transceiver's register interface: parameters implemented entirely in firmware (custom FEC, whitening, encryption) are observable only as opaque FIFO payloads.

</details>


### [129] [Verified Pythagorean Composition for Adaptive Cryptographic Games: Noise Flooding in Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13846)
*Yi Lee,Alexandru Cojocaru,Junyi Liu,Xiaodi Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文使用Rocq和SSProve证明助手对近似同态加密中噪声泛滥（noise flooding）防御的自适应解密攻击安全性进行了机器验证。针对q查询IND-CPAD攻击者，形式化证明了安全界为β_CPA(B)+√(qn)/(2γ)，通过构建新的关系程序逻辑，将条件KL散度预算进行毕达哥拉斯式组合，避免逐次混合论证导致的线性损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 噪声泛滥是近似同态加密应对解密攻击的标准防御手段，但其安全性证明对组合极其敏感。普通混合论证在q次自适应解密回答中会遭受q次线性损失，而密码学证明通过累积条件KL散度并在最后一次性转换为统计距离，才能获得参数关键的平方根损失。手工证明容易出错，需要机器验证来确保正确性。

Method: 利用Rocq和SSProve证明助理，构建一个新的关系程序逻辑框架。关键创新包括：毕达哥拉斯式判断（Pythagorean judgment）允许在不转换为统计距离的情况下组合条件KL预算；验证过的迹编译器（trace compiler）将局部预言机规则提升到任意自适应程序，仅在最终做一次统计距离转换。

Result: 在任意满足近似正确性和IND-CPA安全性的全同态加密方案基础上，形式化证明了对于每一个q查询IND-CPAD攻击者，其优势≤β_CPA(B_{A,q})+√(qn)/(2γ)，其中n为明文维度，γ为泛滥宽度乘数。

Conclusion: 该工作首次对噪声泛滥安全性的棘手组合论证进行了完整的机器化验证，证明了密码学手写证明中条件KL累积与单次统计距离转换的策略是正确可行的，为近似同态加密的安全证明提供了可靠的机械化基础。

Abstract: Noise flooding is a standard defense against decryption attacks on approximate homomorphic encryption, but its security proof is unusually sensitive to composition. Replacing each of $q$ adaptive decryption answers with a statistically close simulation and applying an ordinary hybrid argument loses linearly in $q$. The cryptographic proof instead accumulates conditional Kullback-Leibler (KL) costs and converts to statistical distance once, giving the parameter-critical square-root loss.
  We machine-check this argument using Rocq and SSProve. Given any fully homomorphic encryption scheme that is approximately correct and IND-CPA secure, we formalize a reduction for every $q$-query IND-CPAD adversary and prove \[
  \Pr[\mathsf{IND\text{-}CPAD}_{\mathsf{NF}}^{\mathcal A}=1]
  \leq β_{\mathsf{CPA}}(\mathcal B_{\mathcal A,q})
  + \frac{\sqrt{qn}}{2γ}. \] where $n$ is the plaintext dimension and $γ$ is the flooding-width multiplier. Our proof constructs a new relational program logic over SSProve semantics. Its Pythagorean judgment composes conditional KL budgets without converting them to statistical distance, and a verified trace compiler lifts a local oracle rule to arbitrary adaptive programs with a single final conversion.

</details>


### [130] [CipherSight: Robust Website Fingerprinting via Record-Resource Semantic Supervision under Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13905)
*Runhan Song,Qiqi Liu,Chuanzhou Pan,Zhenquan Ding,Youquan Xian,Chongru Fan,Lei Cui,Wei Wang,Zhiyu Hao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出CipherSight，一种基于TLS记录的分层框架用于鲁棒的HTTPS网站指纹识别，通过层级架构编码记录级属性、掩码记录建模和细粒度记录级资源标注作为特权监督，在封闭世界和开放世界（时域/地理漂移）下均显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HTTPS网站指纹识别方法主要依赖原始TCP包序列，对传输层伪影敏感，难以捕捉稳定且可泛化的网站表示。实际部署中面临时域和地理变化导致的分布外（OOD）问题，以及开放世界中大量未见网站，导致性能显著下降。

Method: CipherSight采用基于TLS记录的分层架构：（1）从TLS记录而非TCP包序列学习网站表示，联合编码多个记录级属性；（2）层级结构同时捕获流内TLS记录之间的依赖关系和并发流之间的流间交互；（3）通过掩码记录建模（MRM）任务学习上下文流量语义；（4）利用细粒度的记录-资源标注作为特权监督，通过结构感知目标函数和语义蒸馏提升表示鲁棒性。

Result: 在封闭世界设定下，CipherSight在超过2000个网站类别上达到95.41%的准确率；在时域和地理漂移条件下均保持90%以上的准确率，在所有评估基线上持续表现最优。

Conclusion: CipherSight验证了基于TLS记录的分层建模相对于TCP包序列方法的优越性，能够有效应对真实部署中的分布漂移问题，为鲁棒的HTTPS网站指纹识别提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: HTTPS website fingerprinting (WF) aims to identify visited websites from metadata observable in encrypted traffic. However, real-world deployments introduce a significant out-of-distribution (OOD) problem caused by temporal and geographic changes, while previously unseen websites are common in open-world scenarios. Existing methods primarily learn from raw TCP packet sequences and struggle to capture stable and generalizable website representations, resulting in performance degradation under practical conditions.
  We propose CipherSight, a TLS-record-based hierarchical framework for robust HTTPS WF. Unlike existing approaches that rely on TCP packet sequences and are sensitive to transport-layer artifacts, CipherSight learns website representations from TLS records by jointly encoding multiple record-level attributes. It introduces a hierarchical architecture that captures both intra-flow dependencies among TLS records and inter-flow interactions across concurrent flows, enabling the model to exploit structural patterns in HTTPS traffic. Besides, to learn robust representations, CipherSight employs a masked record modeling (MRM) task to capture contextual traffic semantics and leverages fine-grained record-resource annotations as privileged supervision through structure-aware objectives and semantic distillation. Experiments show that CipherSight achieves 95.41% accuracy across more than 2,000 website classes in the closed-world setting and maintains over 90% accuracy under both temporal and geographic drift, consistently outperforming all evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [131] [Characterizing the Variance Envelope: A Multi-Dimensional Analysis of Spectre Telemetry Across Architectures and Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13920)
*Jaya Keshava Chandra Kotha,Jean-Luc Gaudiot*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统性地研究了硬件攻击（如Spectre）在硬件性能计数器（HPC）指标中的特征变异性，提出了"方差包络"概念，并在Intel、ARM和AMD三大架构上进行了大规模实验，证明HPC攻击签名高度脆弱且易受执行环境影响，最终论证需要架构感知的自适应监控而非静态检测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的HPC攻击检测方法仅在受控环境下有效，面对真实世界中的系统背景噪声、不同攻击变体和对抗性流量节拍时，静态模型会失效。因此需要系统性地表征攻击签名在不同条件下的变化范围，以弥补这一研究空白。

Method: 作者提出"方差包络"概念来刻画攻击签名在所有可能环境条件下偏移的完整范围。搭建了涵盖Intel、ARM和AMD三种架构的实验矩阵，包含三种攻击变体、四种流量节拍模式和四种背景噪声条件，对各架构组合进行系统性评估。

Result: 实验证明HPC签名高度脆弱，极易被执行环境扭曲。此外，发现了AMD Jaguar处理器的关键微架构瓶颈：Prime+Probe攻击在该硬件上存在持久的硬件级失效，无法正常执行。

Conclusion: 可靠的运行时攻击检测需要架构感知的自适应监控方案，而非依赖静态检测模型。该研究为未来开发跨平台的鲁棒性硬件安全检测系统提供了重要依据。

Abstract: Hardware attacks like Spectre exploit built-in processor vulnerabilities, leaving anomalous footprints in Hardware Performance Counter (HPC) metrics. While machine learning can detect these footprints in controlled settings, static models fail in the real world when confronted with background system noise, diverse attack variants, and adversarial traffic pacing. To close this gap, this paper characterizes the "variance envelope"-the full range of how attack signatures shift- across Intel, ARM, and AMD architectures. We evaluate an extensive experimental matrix encompassing three attack variants, four pacing modes, and four background-noise conditions. Our analysis proves that HPC signatures are highly fragile and easily warped by their execution environment. Furthermore, we expose a critical microarchitectural bottleneck: the persistent, hardware-level failure of Prime+Probe attacks on the AMD Jaguar. Ultimately, this comprehensive characterization demonstrates that reliable runtime detection requires architecture-aware, adaptive monitoring rather than static models.

</details>


### [132] [CoSA: Context-Aware Severity Assessment via Context Analysis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13928)
*Jinfeng Jiang,Yikun Li,Chengran Yang,Ting Zhang,Wen Bin Leow,Yide Yin,Eng Lieh Ouh,Lwin Khin Shar,David Lo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CoSA（Context-aware vulnerability Severity Assessment），一种基于仓库级上下文感知的CVSS基础指标自动评估方法。CoSA构建代码属性图（CPG），通过两阶段仓库剪枝策略（轻量静态剪枝+智能体LLM引导剪枝）提取CVSS相关上下文，生成指标级文本摘要并输入轻量级transformer预测器进行严重性评估。实验表明CoSA在准确率和Macro-F1上分别比最优基线提升14.4%和15.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动评估CVSS基础指标耗时费力，现有自动化方法往往无法捕捉评估许多CVSS基础指标所需的仓库级证据。相关证据分散在整个仓库中且噪声严重，使得仓库感知的漏洞严重性评估具有挑战性。

Method: CoSA包含三个核心组件：(1) 构建代码属性图（CPG）表示仓库结构；(2) 两阶段仓库剪枝策略——先进行轻量静态剪枝保留结构上邻近的上下文，再用智能体LLM引导的剪枝保留CVSS相关上下文并收集支撑证据；(3) 将检索到的仓库上下文整合为紧凑的CVSS指标级文本摘要，输入轻量级transformer预测器进行指标预测。同时构建了包含6,816个CVSS标注样本、覆盖90种CWE类型的高质量仓库级数据集。

Result: 在真实漏洞上的实验表明，CoSA一致优于函数级和纯LLM基线。相比最优基线，预测准确率提升14.4%，Macro-F1提升15.3%。

Conclusion: 显式的、面向指标驱动的仓库上下文检索对于实用且可靠的自动化漏洞严重性评估至关重要。CoSA证明了仓库级上下文感知方法在CVSS自动化评估中的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Accurate vulnerability severity assessment is essential for prioritizing remediation, yet manually assessing Common Vulnerability Scoring System (CVSS) base metrics remains labor-intensive. Existing automated approaches often fail to capture the repository-level evidence required for assessing many CVSS base metrics. Such repository-aware assessment is challenging because relevant evidence is scattered across the entire repository under heavy noise.
  To address these challenges, we present CoSA, a Context-aware vulnerability Severity Assessment approach that infers CVSS base metrics from repository artifacts. CoSA constructs a code property graph (CPG) and applies a two-stage repository-pruning strategy: lightweight static pruning to preserve structurally proximal context, followed by an agentic large language model (LLM)-guided pruning step to retain CVSS-relevant context while collecting supporting evidence. The LLM then consolidates the retrieved repository context into compact, CVSS metric-wise textual summaries, which are fed into a lightweight transformer predictor. We also construct a higher-quality repository-level dataset comprising 6,816 CVSS labeled instances spanning 90 Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) types.
