  Experiments on real-world vulnerabilities show that CoSA consistently outperforms function-level and pure-LLM baselines. It improves prediction accuracy by 14.4% and Macro-F1 by 15.3% over the best-performing baseline, suggesting that explicit, metric-oriented repository context retrieval is crucial for practical and reliable automated severity assessment.

</details>


### [133] [Extracting and Verifying Illicit Bitcoin Addresses from Underground Forum Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13930)
*Abdoul Nasser Hassane Amadou,Arnaud Legout,Imane Fouad,Konstantin Avrachenkov,Anas Motii*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可复现的比特币标签构建管道，从地下网络犯罪论坛HackForums中提取有证据支持的非法比特币地址数据集，包含2,438个经人工验证的地址，覆盖2010-2024年和12类网络犯罪类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的比特币标签数据集大多来源于社区举报、区块链启发式规则、事件特定收集或专有标注流程，这些构建方法通常难以公开复现，且缺乏地址直接参与非法活动的充分证据。缺乏可复现且有证据支持的标签数据集限制了加密货币犯罪研究的可靠性和可验证性。

Method: 该管道结合了LLM辅助筛查、专家审查和链上验证三个环节。首先利用大语言模型从HackForums论坛的十五年存档活动中筛选出可能涉及非法交易的讨论，然后由专家审查确认，最后通过链上验证确认比特币地址与论坛中讨论的非法交易直接关联。每个发布的标签都附带地下讨论的上下文证据并经过链上确认。

Result: 最终数据集包含2,438个经人工验证的非法比特币地址，时间跨度从2010年到2024年，涵盖12类网络犯罪类别。数据集伴随时间元数据和完整的提取管道一起公开发布。

Conclusion: 该工作为加密货币犯罪研究提供了可复现、有证据支持的比特币标签数据集和完整提取流程，弥补了现有数据集缺乏可复现性和直接参与证据的不足，支持可复现的加密货币相关网络犯罪研究。

Abstract: Existing labeled Bitcoin datasets are largely derived from community-reported abuse, blockchain heuristics, incident-specific collections, or proprietary labeling processes. Their construction methods are rarely publicly reproducible and often provide limited evidence that an address was directly involved in illicit activity. We present a reproducible pipeline for constructing evidence-backed Bitcoin labels from HackForums, an underground cybercrime forum with fifteen years of archived activity. The pipeline combines LLM-assisted screening, expert review, and on-chain validation to identify Bitcoin addresses explicitly associated with illicit transactions discussed on the forum. Each released label is supported by contextual evidence from underground discussions and validated on-chain. The resulting dataset contains 2,438 manually verified illicit Bitcoin addresses spanning 2010-2024 and twelve cybercrime categories assigned during LLM screening. We release the dataset, temporal metadata, and the complete extraction pipeline to support reproducible research on cryptocurrency-facilitated cybercrime.

</details>


### [134] [Vectorized SQIsign Implementation Using AVX-512](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13948)
*Weize Wang,Chutong Wang,Yu Wu,Qifan Xue,Jieyu Zheng,Yunlei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个基于AVX-512 IFMA指令集架构的SQIsign完整向量化实现，通过系统性重新设计计算栈（素数域/扩域运算、椭圆曲线运算、配对计算等），结合Qlapoti技术在NIST安全级别I下实现了签名2.69倍、验证3.18倍的加速比，并论证了该向量化策略在AVX10架构下的长期适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SQIsign是唯一入选NIST后量子密码标准化流程的基于同源的数字签名方案，具有密钥和签名尺寸紧凑的优点，但实际部署受限于计算密集的签名过程。此前缺乏针对该方案的系统性SIMD向量化优化研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 系统性地重新设计SQIsign的整个计算栈，包括：(1) 素数域和扩域算术；(2) 椭圆曲线运算，涵盖批量点加法和标量乘法；(3) 通过cubical算术和二维同源求值的配对计算。全部利用AVX-512 IFMA指令集实现向量化，并与Qlapoti技术结合。

Result: 在NIST安全级别I下，与参考实现相比，签名操作获得2.69倍加速，验证操作获得3.18倍加速。向量化策略具有架构无关性，可在更广泛的基于同源的密码学构造中推广应用。

Conclusion: SIMD向量化是后量子同源方案中关键但尚未充分探索的优化维度，与算法层面的进展相互独立。AVX-512并未过时——Intel AVX10（修订版10.2，预计2026年底广泛部署）将在性能核和效率核上标准化AVX-512（含IFMA）能力，确保这些优化技术的长期有效性。

Abstract: SQIsign is the sole isogeny-based digital signature scheme submitted to the NIST Post-Quantum Cryptography standardization process, distinguished by its foundation on the hardness of the endomorphism ring problem for supersingular elliptic curves. While offering compact key and signature sizes, SQIsign's practical deployment is hindered by computationally intensive signing procedures. This paper presents the first comprehensive vectorized implementation of SQIsign utilizing the AVX-512 Integer Fused Multiply-Add (IFMA) instruction set architecture. By systematically redesigning the computational stack---encompassing prime-field and extension-field arithmetic, elliptic curve operations including batched point doubling and scalar multiplication, as well as pairing computations via cubical arithmetic and two-dimensional isogeny evaluations---we achieve substantial performance improvements over the reference implementation. When combined with Qlapoti technology, our implementation attains a $2.69\times$ speedup for signing and a $3.18\times$ improvement for verification at NIST security level I.
  Contrary to misconceptions regarding the obsolescence of AVX-512, we emphasize that Intel's AVX10 instruction set architecture (revision 10.2, scheduled for widespread deployment in late 2026) will standardize AVX-512 capabilities---including IFMA instructions---across both performance and efficiency cores, ensuring long-term viability of these optimization techniques. Furthermore, our vectorization strategies are architecture-agnostic and provide a methodological foundation applicable to broader isogeny-based cryptographic constructions. This work demonstrates that SIMD vectorization represents a critical yet underexplored optimization dimension for post-quantum isogeny-based schemes, independent of recent algorithmic advances.

</details>


### [135] [Structural Leakage in Graph Encryption: Attacks and Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13981)
*Hua Shen,Renzhi Chen,Ge Wu,Willy Susilo,Jing Chen,Mingwu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文全面分析图加密方案（GES）在单对最短路径（SPSP）查询中的结构泄漏问题，整合两项近期工作：揭示PathGES存在严重的不平衡重轻分解（HLD）缺陷（99%以上令牌-路径映射为一对一），提出Fragment Tree攻击实现10.24%的精确查询恢复，并介绍增强方案BlindGES，通过合并-分割机制和两级多映射索引将一对多映射降至20%以下，同时削减50%设置时间和32%存储开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图加密方案支持图的隐私保护外包，但SPSP查询存在结构性泄漏风险。PathGES等方案声称能抵抗查询恢复攻击，然而其HLD分解在真实数据集上严重失衡，导致大量一对一的令牌-路径映射，为攻击者提供了可利用的结构弱点。作者旨在系统性揭示这些弱点并提出改进方案。

Method: 作者采用系统性分析方法：（1）深入分析PathGES的HLD分解与规范片段编码机制，识别其结构失衡问题；（2）提出Fragment Tree攻击方法，利用结构弱点恢复查询内容；（3）设计BlindGES增强方案，引入Merge-and-Divide机制和两级多映射索引，通过减少一对一映射、优化存储和设置开销来降低泄漏；（4）在七个真实数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 实验结果表明：PathGES在真实数据集中超过99%的令牌-路径映射为一对一，可同时受Falzon-Paterson攻击和侧信道路径长度推断攻击；所提出的Fragment Tree攻击在稀疏图上的精确恢复率达10.24%；BlindGES将一对一映射降至20%以下，设置时间削减50%，存储开销减少32%，路径长度泄漏控制在1%以内。

Conclusion: 该报告系统展示了SPSP查询GES的结构泄漏问题，证明现有方案PathGES存在严重安全漏洞，并提出BlindGES作为改进方案，在安全性、效率和实用性之间取得了更好的平衡。攻击方法、防御机制、安全证明及实验评估为图加密领域提供了全面的参考。

Abstract: Graph encryption schemes (GES) enable secure outsourcing of graph data while supporting efficient queries. This report provides a comprehensive analysis of structural leakage in GES for single-pair shortest path (SPSP) queries, integrating findings from two recent works. First, we analyze PathGES, a scheme designed to resist query recovery attacks through heavy-light decomposition (HLD) and canonical fragment encoding. Our analysis reveals that PathGES suffers from significant imbalances in HLD decomposition, with over 99% of token-path mappings being one-to-one on real-world datasets, enabling both the Falzon-Paterson attack and side-channel inference of path lengths. Second, we present Fragment Tree attack that exploits these structural weaknesses to recover query contents, achieving up to 10.24% exact recovery on sparse graphs. Third, we introduce BlindGES, an enhanced scheme incorporating a Merge-and-Divide mechanism and two-level multimap index that reduces one-to-one mappings to below 20%, cuts setup time by 50%, reduces storage overhead by 32%, and limits path length leakage to under 1%. This report systematically presents attack methodologies, defense mechanisms, security proofs, and experimental evaluations on seven real-world datasets.

</details>


### [136] [P2Skill: Privacy Preserving Skill Distillation for Cloud-Local LLM Inference Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14094)
*Myunghoon Ryu,Geunpyo Park,Sungjoon Lee,XinYu Piao,Jong-Kook Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: P2Skill提出一种基于提示技能的蒸馏方法，让本地小语言模型（SLM）通过技能提示自主完成分解、PII感知路由、释义和重构，无需隐私专用微调或辅助检测器，实现云本地协同LLM推理中的隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的隐私保护方法（如提示扰动、实体掩码、模型微调）可能扭曲上下文语义或需要额外训练成本，无法在云本地LLM推理中有效同时保护用户敏感数据并保持推理质量。

Method: P2Skill通过技能提示引导本地SLM执行四个步骤：分解（decomposition）、PII感知路由（PII-aware routing）、释义（paraphrasing）和重构（reconstruction）。由云端LLM从执行失败中迭代精炼技能，使本地SLM能泛化超越记忆的PII模式，无需隐私专用微调或训练辅助检测器。

Result: 在四领域基准上，P2Skill相比先前基线在隐私保护的推理质量上分别提升了1.69倍和3.66倍。

Conclusion: P2Skill证明提示式技能蒸馏能够在不进行额外训练的情况下，有效实现云本地LLM推理中的PII保护，同时显著提升隐私保护下的推理质量。

Abstract: Cloud-local LLM inference systems have the potential to use the reasoning capability of large cloud models while protecting sensitive user data on personal devices. Cloud-bound requests must exclude personally identifiable information (PII) to prevent external data leakage. Existing privacy-preserving methods rely on prompt perturbation, entity masking, or model fine-tuning, but these approaches may distort contextual semantics or require additional training. This paper proposes P2Skill, a prompt-based skill distillation method in which a local small language model (SLM) autonomously performs decomposition, PII-aware routing, paraphrasing, and reconstruction by following the skill prompts. Skills are iteratively refined from execution failures by a cloud LLM, enabling the local SLM to generalize beyond memorized PII patterns, and therefore P2Skill requires no privacy-specific fine-tuning or learned auxiliary detectors. Evaluation on a four-domain benchmark shows that P2Skill achieves $1.69\times$ and $3.66\times$ higher privacy-preserved inference quality than previous baselines.

</details>


### [137] [BGA: A noise-immune neural distillation framework for malicious signature extraction in high-entropy encrypted flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14126)
*Sheng Hong,Yixuan Huang,Weiwei Jiang,Junyuan Zhang,Jiacheng Wang,Ruijian Jiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出BGA框架，一种噪声免疫的神经蒸馏框架，用于缓解TLS 1.3高熵流量中的注意力稀释问题，通过ANOVA特征解耦、WGAN-GP过采样和带门控注意力机制的BiLSTM架构，在工业边缘网关实现实时加密威胁检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TLS 1.3高熵加密流中注意力机制易被稀释，且工业控制平面特征与密码学噪声难以区分，加之恶意样本极度类别不平衡，导致传统深度学习方法在加密威胁情报检测中性能下降。

Method: 1) ANOVA解耦高判别性控制平面特征（工业设定值）与随机密码学噪声；2) WGAN-GP模块（满足1-Lipschitz约束）合成高质量少数类样本以缓解极端类别不平衡；3) 核心架构集成BiLSTM提取时间依赖，并引入自适应门控多头注意力机制作为神经滤波器动态抑制加密伪影、放大恶意签名。

Result: 在CIC-IDS-2018和Edge-IIoT基准上所有关键指标超过95.2%；MSCI攻击检测召回率提升43.2%；噪声注入压力测试中相比vanilla Transformer有8.57%的结构韧性优势；推理延迟0.2820ms（ARM理论缩放估计1.6920ms），证明实时可行。

Conclusion: BGA为异构工业边缘网关上的实时加密威胁检测提供了有前景的架构基线，具有高效的结构韧性和低延迟优势，可作为未来硬件实现的基础。

Abstract: To mitigate attention dilution in high-entropy TLS 1.3 flows, we propose BGA, a noise-immune neural distillation framework for encrypted threat intelligence.The methodology first employs Analysis of Variance (ANOVA) to decouple high-discriminatory control-plane features - specifically industrial setpoints - from stochastic cryptographic noise. To resolve the extreme class imbalance within a corpus of 86,878 flow records, a Wasserstein GAN with Gradient Penalty (WGAN-GP) module, enforcing the 1-Lipschitz constraint, is integrated to synthesize high-fidelity minority samples, elevating the detection recall of rare Malicious State Command Injections(MSCI) attacks by 43.2%. At its core, the BGA architecture integrates Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) for temporal dependency extraction and an Adaptive Gated Multi-Head Attention mechanism. This gated unit functions as a neural filter to dynamically suppress encryption artifacts while amplifying malicious signatures. Extensive evaluations on CIC-IDS-2018 and Edge-IIoT benchmarks demonstrate a performance ceiling exceeding 95.2% across all key metrics. Furthermore, noise-injection stress tests confirm BGAs superior structural resilience with a 8.57% performance margin over vanilla Transformers, while its ultra-low inference latency of 0.2820 ms (estimated 1.6920 ms via theoretical scaling for ARM) indicates a high potential for real-time feasibility on heterogeneous industrial edge gateways, providing a promising architectural baseline for future hardware implementation.

</details>


### [138] [MazeRunner: Nonlinear Task and Clue Orchestration for LLM-driven Black-Box Automated Penetration Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14216)
*Zhenyuan Li,Yi Jiang,Junjie Cheng,Yaokun Li,Jing Qiu,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MazeRunner是一个基于三智能体任务与线索编排框架的自主渗透测试系统，通过分离全局编排、上下文执行和失败审查，并用持久性维护任务状态与环境证据，解决真实黑盒测试中攻击图未知、分支多、失败模糊及长时距线索关联等非线性问题。在10个HTB目标上以Claude Sonnet 4.5作为后端模型，完成47.7%的标注子任务（对比PentestGPT-V2的36.2%和Claude Code的34.2%），6个目标达到用户级以上权限，其中2个获得root权限，而基线模型均无法获得root权限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM驱动的渗透测试智能体主要在简化的线性场景中执行端到端工作流，但真实世界的黑盒测试本质上是非线性的：攻击图初始未知需从环境反馈中逐步推断，观察可能揭示多条攻击分支，失败往往具有歧义性，关键线索可能跨越很长的行动时距。这些特性导致现有智能体容易陷入深度优先探索的陷阱、误判失败原因并遗忘先前的证据。

Method: MazeRunner构建了一个三智能体任务与线索编排框架：分离全局编排（global orchestration）、上下文密集型执行（context-intensive execution）和面向失败的审查（failure-oriented review），同时持久化维护任务状态和环境证据。该设计支持动作修订、前置条件恢复、分支切换和长距离线索关联。

Result: 在10个近期发布的HTB目标上评估，每个系统-目标运行限制为2000万LLM token并防止目标特定解决方案泄露。使用Claude Sonnet 4.5时，MazeRunner完成47.7%的标注子任务，优于PentestGPT-V2（36.2%）和Claude Code（34.2%）。MazeRunner在6个目标上达到用户级或更高权限（其中2个获得root权限），而同模型的基线仅在2个目标上达到用户级权限且从未获得root权限。执行轨迹分析显示MazeRunner能探索更多攻击分支并更高效地获取shell。

Conclusion: 通过将编排、执行和审查职责分离并持久化维护任务状态与环境证据，MazeRunner有效解决了黑盒渗透测试的非线性挑战，显著优于现有端到端的LLM渗透测试智能体，在子任务完成率、权限提升深度和攻击分支探索效率等多个维度均展现出明显优势。

Abstract: Penetration testing is essential yet resource-intensive. Although large language models (LLMs) show promise for automating security auditing, existing agents mainly execute end-to-end workflows in simplified linear scenarios. Real-world black-box testing is fundamentally nonlinear: the attack graph is initially unknown and must be incrementally inferred from environmental feedback. Observations may reveal multiple attack branches, failures are often ambiguous, and critical clues may span long action horizons. Existing agents therefore tend to become trapped in depth-first exploration, misdiagnose failures, and forget prior evidence. We present MazeRunner, an autonomous penetration testing system built on a three-agent task-and-clue orchestration framework. It separates global orchestration, context-intensive execution, and failure-oriented review while persistently maintaining task states and environmental evidence. This design supports action revision, prerequisite recovery, branch switching, and long-range clue correlation. We evaluate MazeRunner on 10 recently released HTB targets, limiting each system-target run to 20 million LLM tokens and preventing target-specific solution leakage. With Claude Sonnet 4.5, MazeRunner completes 47.7% of annotated subtasks, compared with 36.2% for PentestGPT-V2 and 34.2% for Claude Code. It achieves user-level or higher access on six targets, including root access on two; each same-model baseline reaches user-level access on only two targets and never obtains root access. Execution-trace analysis further shows that MazeRunner explores more attack branches and acquires shells more efficiently.

</details>


### [139] [A Four-Axis Trustworthiness Benchmark for LLM-as-Judge in Principle-Based Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14329)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文反对将基于原则的监管（如"公平、清晰、不误导"等评价性标准）简化为二元谓词，主张LLM-as-judge必须从准确性、释义鲁棒性、对抗鲁棒性和校准四个维度评估。作者发布Principle-Bench基准（168个加密资产金融推广场景，映射至英国FCA两大原则），并引入Ceca（校准的范例聚类评估器），可输出逐样本反事实归因。实验发现没有方法在所有四个轴上全面占优，120B LLM-judge在良性输入上最强，但在关键词堆砌的Consumer Duty输入上精度从0.74暴跌至0.27（"合规剧场"），且不同模型家族对同一分割的一致性仅为Cohen's kappa=0.16，定位了失败源于模型而非语料。结论是任何部署级LLM-judge必须报告逐原则对抗欺骗和事后校准指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于原则的监管（如"公平、清晰且不误导"或"交付良好结果"）本质上是评价性标准，无法简化为二元谓词，因此LLM-as-judge被越来越多地用作替代方案。然而，目前缺乏对这些评判模型的系统评估框架，导致其可靠性无法得到保障。

Method: 构建Principle-Bench基准，包含168个加密资产金融推广场景，映射到英国FCA的两项原则，并在预注册规则下生成释义扰动、对抗性关键词堆砌和边界扰动。提出Ceca（Calibrated Exemplar-Cluster Assessment），一种可校准、可审计的评估器，能输出每个样本的精确反事实归因。对比了关键词计数、三种sentence-transformer嵌入器、开源权重LLM-judge和校准级联等多种方法。

Result: 没有任何方法能在四个评估轴上全面占优。最强的120B LLM-judge在良性输入上表现最佳，但在关键词堆砌的Consumer Duty输入上精度从0.74骤降至0.27，被认定为"合规剧场"（compliance theatre）。另一模型家族的第二位评判者在同一分割上与前者的一致性仅为Cohen's kappa=0.16，将失败定位为模型本身而非语料问题。

Conclusion: 任何可用于部署的LLM-judge在基于原则的监管场景中，都必须报告逐原则的对抗性欺骗指标和事后校准指标，不能仅依赖聚合准确率。基准和评估框架为原则性监管的AI评判提供了全面的四轴评估范式。

Abstract: Principle-based regulation, with evaluative standards such as "fair, clear, and not misleading" or "deliver good outcomes", cannot be reduced to binary predicates, and LLM-as-judge is increasingly used as the substitute. Our position is that any such judge must be evaluated on four axes: accuracy, paraphrase robustness, adversarial robustness, and calibration. We release Principle-Bench, 168 cryptoasset financial-promotion scenarios mapped to two UK FCA principles, with paraphrase, adversarial keyword-stuffing, and boundary perturbations authored under a pre-registered rubric; the first benchmark covering all four axes for principle-based regulation. We also introduce Ceca (Calibrated Exemplar-Cluster Assessment): a calibrated, auditable assessor that emits exact per-exemplar counterfactual attributions. Across keyword counting, three sentence-transformer embedders, an open-weight LLM-judge, and a calibrated cascade, no method dominates all four axes. A 120B LLM-judge, strongest on benign inputs, loses 47 accuracy points (0.74 to 0.27) on keyword-stuffed Consumer Duty inputs: "compliance theatre." A second judge from a different model family agrees only at Cohen's kappa = 0.16 on that split, localising the failure to the model rather than the corpus. Any deployment-grade LLM-judge for principle-based regulation must report per-principle adversarial deception and post-hoc calibration alongside aggregate accuracy.

</details>


### [140] [A Hybrid LLM-Based Framework for Automated Security Annotation Generation in Business Process Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14370)
*Md Kamrul Islam,Tiphaine Henry,Mattia Salnitri,Julius Köpke,Sami Souihi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个混合框架，结合LLM语义提取、模式约束映射、规则归一化和确定性验证，自动从自然语言安全需求文档中为BPMN流程模型生成符合SecBPMN2规范的注解。在27个流程模型上评估，生成注解的结构有效性和模式完整性高，精度远超人工分析师（0.58 vs 0.29），召回率相当（0.52 vs 0.50），错误注解减少近50%，且生成速度显著更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全业务流程的建模与分析需要将安全注解融入流程模型，但现有BPMN扩展（如SecBPMN2）的注解推导依赖手工专家操作，既耗时又易出错，缺乏自动化手段。

Method: 构建混合框架，输入为BPMN流程模型和安全需求文档，通过LLM语义提取、模式约束映射、规则归一化及确定性验证四个阶段的组合，自动生成符合SecBPMN2规范的安全注解。

Result: 在27个跨域流程模型的数据集上评估，系统持续生成结构有效且模式完整度高的SecBPMN2注解；精度0.58显著高于人工0.29，召回率0.52与人工0.50相当，错误或错位注解减少近50%，生成速度远超手工标注。

Conclusion: 混合LLM与规则自动化能够在降低建模工作量的同时提升一致性和可靠性，为安全设计BPM提供可扩展的基础。

Abstract: The modelling and analysis of secure business processes require the incorporation of security annotations into process models. Although BPMN extensions, including SecBPMN2, exist for this purpose, the derivation of accurate and complete security annotations from natural-language specifications remains a manual, expert-intensive, and error-prone task. This paper presents a hybrid framework that takes a BPMN process model and a security requirements document as input and automatically generates security annotations adhering to the SecBPMN2 specification. The approach combines Large Language Model (LLM)--based semantic extraction with schema-constrained mapping, rule-based normalization, and deterministic validation. The framework is evaluated comprehensively on a curated dataset of 27 process models from various domains. The results indicate that it consistently produces structurally valid SecBPMN2 annotations with high schema completeness. Compared to human security analysts, the system achieves substantially higher precision (0.58 vs. 0.29) while maintaining comparable recall (0.52 vs. 0.50) and reduces erroneous or misplaced annotations by nearly 50%. In addition, annotation generation is significantly faster than manual annotation. These findings demonstrate that hybrid LLM- and rule-based automation can reduce modeling effort while improving consistency and reliability, thereby providing a scalable foundation for security-by-design BPM.

</details>


### [141] [STINER: Automated Extraction of Strategic Cyber Threat Intelligence from X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14418)
*Yasir Ech-Chammakhy,Oussama Azrara,Jaafar Chbili,Anas Motii*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出STINER，一个用于从社交媒体（X/Twitter）流中提取战略级网络威胁情报（CTI）的专家标注语料库与细粒度实体分类体系，涵盖8种实体类型和2,100条真实告警。在12个配置下基准测试了9种模型，DarkBERT域适应编码器达到89.33%的严格F1分数，优于通用编码器和微调大语言模型。基于STINER-DarkBERT进行的2025年上半年欧洲威胁格局分析，揭示了西班牙攻击的独特可见性特征，并在SafePay勒索软件被厂商报告回溯之前成功捕获了该活动的早期信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体（特别是X平台）已成为战略级网络威胁情报的最快来源，常比厂商正式报告提前数天发布漏洞通告。然而，社交媒体对话的非正式和高度不规则语言特征使传统命名实体识别（NER）模型难以解析，造成自动化防御系统的盲区。现有方法缺乏针对战略级CTI（如威胁组织、目标行业、数据损失规模等高层级洞察）的专用语料库与实体抽取方案。

Method: 构建STINER语料库：1) 提出以战略枢轴为中心的8种细粒度实体类型分类体系（包括威胁行为者、行业、地点等）；2) 专家标注2,100条真实社交媒体告警数据；3) 在12个配置下基准测试9种模型，涵盖通用编码器、领域适应编码器（如DarkBERT）、开放模式抽取及生成式LLM（零样本与微调）；4) 基于最优模型STINER-DarkBERT进行欧洲威胁格局分析。

Result: 领域适应编码器DarkBERT达到89.33%的严格F1分数，超越通用基线和微调LLM；微调LLM还带来显著更高的推理延迟。欧洲威胁格局分析结果与官方报告在主要目标上保持一致，同时揭示了西班牙攻击的独特可见性特征，并能提前于厂商回溯性报告捕捉到SafePay勒索软件活动的早期信号。

Conclusion: STINER验证了领域适应编码器在社交媒体战略CTI提取任务上的优越性能，证明了基于社交媒体驱动的实体抽取能够提供官方厂商报告之外的时间领先优势，为自动化的战略级威胁情报获取与早期预警提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Strategic Cyber Threat Intelligence (CTI) focuses on high-level insights, such as identifying targeted industries, attributing attacks to specific ransomware groups, and assessing the scale of data loss. Today, X (formerly Twitter) has become the fastest source for this intelligence, often hosting real-time breach announcements days before formal vendor reports. Converting this raw chatter into actionable intelligence requires navigating a complex linguistic landscape. Conventional Named Entity Recognition (NER) models struggle to parse the informal and highly irregular dialect of social media, creating a blind spot for automated defense systems. To address this challenge, we introduce STINER, a taxonomy and expert-annotated corpus for extracting strategic intelligence from social media streams. We construct a high-quality, expert-annotated dataset of 2,100 real-world alerts and propose a granular taxonomy of eight entity types centered on strategic pivots such as Threat Actor, Sector, and Location. We benchmark nine models across 12 evaluated configurations, spanning general-purpose and domain-adapted encoders, open-schema extraction, and generative LLMs in both zero-shot and fine-tuned settings. Domain-adapted encoders such as DarkBERT reach a strict F1-score of 89.33%, outperforming both general-purpose baselines and fine-tuned Large Language Models, which additionally incur substantially higher inference latency. Leveraging STINER-DarkBERT, we conduct a European threat landscape analysis for H1 2025. Our results align with official reporting on major targets while highlighting the distinct visibility profile of attacks in Spain, and illustrate how social-media-driven extraction can surface early signals of the SafePay ransomware campaign prior to its retrospective characterization in vendor threat landscape reports.

</details>


### [142] [Lower Bounds on Black-Box Constructions of Pseudorandom Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14501)
*Bar Alon,Itai Dinur,Muthuramakrishnan Venkitasubramaniam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了从伪随机生成器（PRG）构建伪随机函数（PRF）的完全黑盒构造的下界问题。作者证明了在没有自适应调用的情况下，任何这样的构造至少需要ω(n/log n)次（对于弱PRF为ω(in/log in)次）PRG调用，这推广了GGM构造在此类黑盒框架下的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GGM构造（Goldreich-Goldwasser-Micali，1984）是从PRG构建PRF的经典方法，需要ω(log n)次PRG调用。Beimel等人（2024）证明了在树构造这一特定家族中GGM是最优的，但更一般的问题——是否能仅用一次PRG调用构建PRF——仍然悬而未决。本文旨在通过更强的下界来回答这一基本挑战。

Method: 作者考虑了完全黑盒构造（构造和归约都是黑盒的），其中归约与敌手交互的次数独立于敌手每次交互中对其底层函数的调用次数。在此框架下，他们证明了不存在使用o(n/log n)次非自适应PRG调用的构造，对于弱PRF（输入长度为in）则为o(in/log in)。证明还扩展到允许自适应查询但具有足够长输出的弱PRF下界。

Result: 主要结果表明：在完全黑盒框架下，任何从PRG构造PRF的构造至少需要ω(n/log n)次非自适应PRG调用（其中n为PRG的输入长度），对于弱PRF则为ω(in/log in)次。该不可能性结果甚至在敌手仅进行i.i.d.均匀随机查询且PRF仅输出一位的情况下也成立。此外，对于输出足够长的弱PRF，即使允许自适应查询，同样存在下界。

Conclusion: 该工作为从PRG黑盒构造PRF的调用复杂度提供了新的下界，在更广泛的构造类（不限于树构造）中确认了GGM构造几乎是调用最优的，并部分解决了能否仅用一次PRG调用构建PRF的开放问题。

Abstract: In their seminal work, Goldreich, Goldwasser, and Micali [CRYPTO 1984] constructed a pseudorandom function (PRF) using a black-box access to a pseudorandom generator (PRG). When combined with Levin's domain extension technique, the GGM construction invokes the PRG $ω(\log n)$ times, where $n$ denotes the input length to the PRG. To this day, no black-box construction achieving fewer calls is known.
  Recently, Beimel, Malkin, and Mazor [CRYPTO 2024] showed that for a certain family of constructions, which they termed \emph{tree constructions}, the GGM construction is optimal. However, the basic challenge of whether a PRF can be built with just \emph{one invocation} of the PRG still remains open.
  In this work, we consider fully black-box constructions of PRFs from PRGs, where both the construction and the reduction are required to be black-box, and the number of interactions the reduction makes with the adversary is independent of the number of oracle calls the adversary makes to its underlying function within each interaction. Our main result shows that no such construction can have $o(n/\log n)$ and $o(\mathsf{in}/\log\mathsf{in})$ \emph{non-adaptive} calls to the PRG, where $\mathsf{in}$ is the input length of the PRF. This impossibility holds even for weak PRFs with one-bit output, where the adversary is restricted to making i.i.d. uniformly random queries. In addition, we prove a lower bound for weak PRFs with sufficiently long outputs that holds even when the construction is allowed to make adaptive queries to the PRG.

</details>


### [143] [Trust Without Boundaries: An Architectural Analysis of Satellite Flight Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14532)
*Jack Vanlyssel,Gruia-Catalin Roman,Kendra Cook,Sazzadur Rahaman,Afsah Anwar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了NASA核心飞行软件(cFS)的架构安全弱点，通过NOS3模拟器中的五个实验证明了单一受攻击组件可滥用合法架构权限，难以与正常行为区分，并与其他模块化飞行软件框架对比，提出加强内部信任边界的架构改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着航天器软件化和互联化程度加深，机载飞行软件成为日益重要的安全边界。然而主流飞行软件架构常将机载组件视为可信对等体，简化了集成但限制了内部隔离和访问控制，亟需审视其内在信任假设和安全弱点。

Method: 系统分析NASA核心飞行软件(cFS)中权威、身份、通信、可观测性和持久性在机载组件间的分布方式；利用NASA飞行代表性的NOS3模拟器，通过五个恶意机载组件实验（滥用合法架构权限）验证安全弱点；并将cFS与其他模块化飞行软件框架进行对比，识别重复出现的信任假设和架构弱点。

Result: 实验表明，单个受损组件能够利用广泛共享的权威执行恶意行为，且这些行为与合法行为难以区分；通过与其他模块化飞行软件的对比，识别出多框架共同存在的架构级信任假设缺陷。

Conclusion: 论文总结了架构层面的启示，并讨论了在未来的飞行软件系统中加强内部信任边界的机制，建议通过更严格的授权、隔离和可观测性设计来提升安全性。

Abstract: As spacecraft become more software-driven and interconnected, onboard flight software is an increasingly important security boundary. Popular flight software architectures often treat onboard components as trusted peers, simplifying integration while limiting internal isolation and access control. We analyze NASA's Core Flight Software (cFS) to examine how authority, identity, communication, observability, and persistence are distributed across onboard components. Using NASA's flight-representative NOS3 simulator, we validate these weaknesses through five experiments implemented with a malicious onboard component that abuses legitimate architectural privileges. We then compare cFS with other modular flight software frameworks to identify recurring trust assumptions and architectural weaknesses. Our results show that a single compromised component can exploit broadly shared authority in ways that are difficult to distinguish from legitimate behavior. We conclude with architectural implications and discuss mechanisms for strengthening internal trust boundaries in future flight software systems.

</details>


### [144] [Finding Vulnerabilities via LLM-Augmented Semantics-Aware Type-Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14533)
*Ruizhe Wang,Meng Xu,N. Asokan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SETYPE，一个基于语义感知的类型系统，利用LLM从源代码中提取符号和表达式的自然语言语义进行类型推断和检查，类型检查失败即表示潜在漏洞。原型PYSETYPE在Python Web应用上实现87%的检测精度和88%的准确率，发现15个潜在零日漏洞，其中9个被开发者确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态分析漏洞检测依赖安全专家手工编码不安全模式规则，但这种方法主要关注语法模式，忽视了代码中更深层的语义信息（如变量和函数名的含义）。随着软件系统日益复杂，仅用语法规则建模漏洞变得越来越困难。

Method: 提出SETYPE——一种语义感知的类型系统，可直接从源代码中基于符号和表达式在自然语言中的含义推导得出。在该类型系统中，类型推断和类型检查均由大语言模型（LLM）执行，类型检查失败即指示潜在漏洞。并实现了原型PYSETYPE用于检测Python Web应用中的漏洞。

Result: 在真实世界应用上的评估达到87%的检测精度和88%的检测准确率。使用PYSETYPE识别出15个潜在的零日漏洞，其中9个已被开发者确认。

Conclusion: SETYPE通过利用LLM理解代码语义的方式，弥补了传统基于语法规则的方法在漏洞检测中的局限性，证明了语义感知方法在软件漏洞检测中的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Vulnerability detection via static analysis traditionally relies on security experts encoding insecure coding patterns into algorithmic rules. However, this approach often focuses on syntactic patterns and overlooks deeper semantic information in the code, such as the meanings of variable and function names. As software systems grow more complex, modeling vulnerabilities using only syntactic rules becomes increasingly challenging.
  In this paper, we propose a semantics-aware approach to detecting software vulnerabilities. We present SETYPE, a semantics-aware type system that can be derived directly from source code based solely on the meanings of symbols and expressions in natural language. In the SETYPE type system, both type inference and checking are performed by Large Language Models (LLMs), and a failed type check indicates a potential vulnerability.
  We prototype PYSETYPE to demonstrate the feasibility of SETYPE for detecting vulnerabilities in Python web applications. Our evaluation on real-world applications achieves 87% detection precision and 88% detection accuracy. Using PYSETYPE, we identified 15 potential zero-day vulnerabilities, nine of which were confirmed by developers.

</details>
